   1 | """
   2 | Tree builder service module.
   3 | 
   4 | Provides functions for phylogenetic tree inference:
   5 | - Neighbor Joining (BioPython)
   6 | - RAxML-NG
   7 | - IQ-TREE
   8 | - MrBayes
   9 | 
  10 | When job_id is provided, streams log output to Redis for real-time SSE updates.
  11 | """
  12 | 
  13 | import os
  14 | import shutil
  15 | import logging
  16 | from pathlib import Path
  17 | from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, Tuple
  18 | 
  19 | from app.config import Config
  20 | from app.models import TreeBuilderParams
  21 | 
  22 | _RAXML_HELP_CACHE = None
  23 | from app.services.subprocess_utils import run_command, run_command_streaming
  24 | from app.services.fasta_utils import sanitize_fasta_headers, restore_tree_names
  25 | 
  26 | # Try to import Bio.Phylo for NJ, or implement simple fallback
  27 | try:
  28 |     from Bio import Phylo, AlignIO, SeqIO
  29 |     from Bio.Phylo.TreeConstruction import DistanceCalculator, DistanceTreeConstructor
  30 |     HAS_BIOPYTHON = True
  31 | except ImportError:
  32 |     HAS_BIOPYTHON = False
  33 | 
  34 | logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
  35 | 
  36 | 
  37 | def run_tree_builder(
  38 |     alignment_fasta: Path,
  39 |     output_newick: Path,
  40 |     output_nexus: Path,
  41 |     params: TreeBuilderParams,
  42 |     config: Config,
  43 |     task_logger,
  44 |     job_id: Optional[str] = None
  45 | ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  46 |     """
  47 |     Run the selected tree building algorithm.
  48 |     
  49 |     Methods: "nj", "raxml", "iqtree", "mrbayes"
  50 |     
  51 |     Args:
  52 |         alignment_fasta: Path to aligned FASTA file
  53 |         output_newick: Path for output Newick tree
  54 |         output_nexus: Path for output NEXUS tree
  55 |         params: Tree builder parameters
  56 |         config: Application config
  57 |         task_logger: Logger instance
  58 |         job_id: Optional job ID for real-time event streaming
  59 |     
  60 |     Returns:
  61 |         Metadata dict with method, model, etc.
  62 |     """
  63 |     method = params.method.lower()
  64 |     task_logger.info(f"Starting tree building with method: {method}")
  65 |     
  66 |     metadata = {
  67 |         "method": method,
  68 |         "model": params.model,
  69 |         "bootstrap": params.bootstrap,
  70 |         "run_dir": str(output_newick.parent)
  71 |     }
  72 | 
  73 |     try:
  74 |         if method == "nj":
  75 |             _run_neighbor_joining(alignment_fasta, output_newick, output_nexus, task_logger, job_id)
  76 |         elif method == "raxml":
  77 |             _run_raxml(alignment_fasta, output_newick, output_nexus, params, config, task_logger, job_id)
  78 |         elif method == "iqtree":
  79 |             _run_iqtree(alignment_fasta, output_newick, output_nexus, params, config, task_logger, job_id)
  80 |         elif method == "mrbayes":
  81 |             _run_mrbayes(alignment_fasta, output_newick, output_nexus, params, config, task_logger, job_id)
  82 |         elif method == "fasttree":
  83 |             _run_fasttree(alignment_fasta, output_newick, output_nexus, params, config, task_logger, job_id)
  84 |         else:
  85 |             raise ValueError(f"Unsupported tree building method: {method}")
  86 | 
  87 |         if not output_newick.exists() or output_newick.stat().st_size == 0:
  88 |              raise RuntimeError(f"Tree building failed: Output file {output_newick} is missing or empty.")
  89 | 
  90 |         task_logger.info(f"Tree building completed successfully. Output: {output_newick}")
  91 |         return metadata
  92 | 
  93 |     except Exception as e:
  94 |         task_logger.error(f"Tree building failed: {e}")
  95 |         raise
  96 | 
  97 | 
  98 | def _get_thread_count(params: TreeBuilderParams) -> int:
  99 |     if params.threads:
 100 |         return params.threads
 101 |     return min(8, os.cpu_count() or 1)
 102 | 
 103 | 
 104 | def _make_log_callback(job_id: Optional[str], step: str, stream: str):
 105 |     """Create a callback function for streaming output to Redis."""
 106 |     if not job_id:
 107 |         return None
 108 |     
 109 |     from app.workers.events import publish_log
 110 |     
 111 |     def callback(line: str):
 112 |         publish_log(job_id, step, stream, line)
 113 |     
 114 |     return callback
 115 | 
 116 | 
 117 | def _run_neighbor_joining(
 118 |     alignment_fasta: Path,
 119 |     output_newick: Path,
 120 |     output_nexus: Path,
 121 |     task_logger,
 122 |     job_id: Optional[str] = None
 123 | ):
 124 |     """
 125 |     Build a fast NJ tree using BioPython.
 126 |     
 127 |     NJ is fast and runs in-process, so no streaming needed.
 128 |     """
 129 |     if not HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 130 |         raise RuntimeError("BioPython is required for NJ but not installed.")
 131 |         
 132 |     task_logger.info("Running Neighbor Joining using BioPython...")
 133 |     
 134 |     # Sanitize input FASTA to create safe IDs
 135 |     sanitized_fasta = output_newick.parent / "nj_input_sanitized.fasta"
 136 |     name_mapping = sanitize_fasta_headers(alignment_fasta, sanitized_fasta)
 137 |     
 138 |     # Publish progress if job_id provided
 139 |     if job_id:
 140 |         from app.workers.events import publish_log
 141 |         publish_log(job_id, "tree", "stderr", "Reading alignment...")
 142 |     
 143 |     # Read alignment (use sanitized version)
 144 |     aln = AlignIO.read(str(sanitized_fasta), "fasta")
 145 |     
 146 |     if job_id:
 147 |         from app.workers.events import publish_log
 148 |         publish_log(job_id, "tree", "stderr", "Calculating distance matrix...")
 149 |     
 150 |     # Calculate distance matrix
 151 |     calculator = DistanceCalculator('identity')
 152 |     dm = calculator.get_distance(aln)
 153 |     
 154 |     if job_id:
 155 |         from app.workers.events import publish_log
 156 |         publish_log(job_id, "tree", "stderr", "Building tree...")
 157 |     
 158 |     # Build tree
 159 |     constructor = DistanceTreeConstructor()
 160 |     tree = constructor.nj(dm)
 161 |     
 162 |     if job_id:
 163 |         from app.workers.events import publish_log
 164 |         publish_log(job_id, "tree", "stderr", "Writing output files...")
 165 |     
 166 |     # Write Newick
 167 |     Phylo.write(tree, str(output_newick), "newick")
 168 |     
 169 |     # Write Nexus
 170 |     Phylo.write(tree, str(output_nexus), "nexus")
 171 |     
 172 |     # Restore original names in output files
 173 |     restore_tree_names(output_newick, name_mapping)
 174 |     restore_tree_names(output_nexus, name_mapping)
 175 | 
 176 | 
 177 | 
 178 | def _check_raxml_feature(config: Config, feature_flag: str) -> bool:
 179 |     """Check if the installed RAxML-NG binary supports a specific flag/feature."""
 180 |     global _RAXML_HELP_CACHE
 181 |     
 182 |     if _RAXML_HELP_CACHE is not None:
 183 |          return feature_flag in _RAXML_HELP_CACHE
 184 | 
 185 |     from app.services.subprocess_utils import run_command
 186 |     try:
 187 |         cmd = [config.RAXML_BINARY, "--help"]
 188 |         _, stdout, _ = run_command(cmd)
 189 |         _RAXML_HELP_CACHE = stdout
 190 |         return feature_flag in stdout
 191 |     except Exception:
 192 |         return False
 193 | 
 194 | 
 195 | 
 196 | 
 197 | def _get_raxml_cmd(
 198 |     params: Any, # ResolvedRaxmlParams
 199 |     config: Config,
 200 |     sanitized_fasta: Path,
 201 |     prefix: str,
 202 |     threads: int
 203 | ) -> list[str]:
 204 |     """Construct the main RAxML-NG command from resolved parameters."""
 205 |     
 206 |     cmd = [
 207 |         config.RAXML_BINARY,
 208 |         "--msa", str(sanitized_fasta),
 209 |         "--model", params.model,
 210 |         "--prefix", prefix,
 211 |         "--threads", str(threads),
 212 |         "--seed", str(params.seed)
 213 |     ]
 214 |     
 215 |     # Starting Trees
 216 |     # raxml-ng --tree pars{N},rand{M} 
 217 |     cmd.extend(["--tree", params.start_tree_spec])
 218 |     
 219 |     # Bootstrapping vs ML-only
 220 |     if params.enable_bootstrap:
 221 |         # --all includes ML search + Bootstrap
 222 |         cmd.append("--all")
 223 |         # --bs-trees autoMRE{cap}
 224 |         cmd.extend(["--bs-trees", f"autoMRE{{{params.bootstrap_cap}}}"])
 225 |     else:
 226 |         # ML check only
 227 |         # Verified ML-only mode: --search
 228 |         cmd.append("--search")
 229 |         
 230 |     # Early Stopping (KH)
 231 |     # Only add if enabled AND supported (checked by caller or we assume caller checked)
 232 |     if params.enable_early_stopping:
 233 |         cmd.extend(["--stop-rule", "kh-mult"]) # or kh, but kh-mult often safer/standard
 234 | 
 235 |     # Outgroup (Drawing option in RAxML, but useful for logs)
 236 |     if params.outgroup:
 237 |         cmd.extend(["--outgroup", params.outgroup])
 238 | 
 239 |     return cmd
 240 | 
 241 | def _run_moose(
 242 |     alignment_fasta: Path,
 243 |     params: Any, # ResolvedRaxmlParams
 244 |     config: Config,
 245 |     task_logger,
 246 |     job_id: str
 247 | ) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Path]:
 248 |     """
 249 |     Run MOOSE model selection.
 250 |     Returns (best_model, alignment_path_to_use).
 251 |     alignment_path_to_use is either the original input or a reduced alignment if MOOSE produced one.
 252 |     """
 253 |     task_logger.info("Running MOOSE model selection...")
 254 |     from app.services.subprocess_utils import run_command
 255 |     
 256 |     prefix = str(alignment_fasta.parent / "moose_run")
 257 |     
 258 |     # Constrained search space
 259 |     # DNA: GTR, HKY, TN93. RHAS: G, I+G
 260 |     # AA: LG, JTT, WAG. RHAS: G, I+G
 261 |     if params.data_type == "DNA":
 262 |         moose_opts = "criterion=bic/rhas=G,R,I+G,I+R/freerate-categories=2-5"
 263 | 
 264 |     else:
 265 |         moose_opts = "criterion=bic/rhas=G,R,I+G,I+R/freerate-categories=2-5/substitution-models=LG,JTT,WAG"
 266 |         
 267 |     cmd = [
 268 |         config.RAXML_BINARY,
 269 |         "--moose",
 270 |         "--msa", str(alignment_fasta),
 271 |         "--data-type", params.data_type,
 272 |         "--moose-options", moose_opts,
 273 |         "--prefix", prefix,
 274 |         "--threads", "auto", 
 275 |         "--seed", str(params.seed)
 276 |     ]
 277 |     
 278 |     if job_id:
 279 |         from app.workers.events import publish_command
 280 |         publish_command(job_id, "tree", cmd)
 281 |     
 282 |     log_file = alignment_fasta.parent.parent / "logs" / "moose.log"
 283 |     rc, stdout, stderr = run_command(cmd, log_file=log_file)
 284 |     
 285 |     if rc != 0:
 286 |         task_logger.warning(f"MOOSE failed (RC={rc}). See moose.log. Falling back to default model.")
 287 |         return None, alignment_fasta
 288 |         
 289 |     # Check for reduced alignment
 290 |     # Wiki says: <prefix>.raxml.reduced.phy
 291 |     reduced_aln = Path(f"{prefix}.raxml.reduced.phy")
 292 |     final_aln = reduced_aln if reduced_aln.exists() else alignment_fasta
 293 |     
 294 |     if reduced_aln.exists():
 295 |         task_logger.info("MOOSE produced reduced alignment. Using it for inference.")
 296 | 
 297 |     # Parse output .bestModel
 298 |     # Wiki says .moose.bestModel
 299 |     best_model_file = Path(f"{prefix}.raxml.moose.bestModel")
 300 |     if not best_model_file.exists():
 301 |         # Fallback to old check just in case
 302 |         best_model_file = Path(f"{prefix}.raxml.bestModel")
 303 | 
 304 |     if best_model_file.exists():
 305 |         # Return the path to the bestModel file directly
 306 |         # RAxML-NG supports reading model from file: --model /path/to/file
 307 |         task_logger.info(f"Using MOOSE model file: {best_model_file}")
 308 |         
 309 |         # We still want to log what it found for the user
 310 |         try:
 311 |              raw_model = best_model_file.read_text().strip()
 312 |              task_logger.info(f"MOOSE selected model configuration: {raw_model}")
 313 |         except Exception:
 314 |              pass
 315 |              
 316 |         return str(best_model_file.absolute()), final_aln
 317 |     
 318 |     task_logger.warning("MOOSE output file not found. Falling back.")
 319 |     return None, alignment_fasta
 320 | 
 321 | def _run_raxml(
 322 |     alignment_fasta: Path,
 323 |     output_newick: Path,
 324 |     output_nexus: Path,
 325 |     params: TreeBuilderParams,
 326 |     config: Config,
 327 |     task_logger,
 328 |     job_id: Optional[str] = None
 329 | ):
 330 |     """Run RAxML-NG tree inference with upgraded workflow."""
 331 |     from app.services.raxml_validator import validate_and_resolve_raxml_params
 332 |     
 333 |     # 1. Detect Data Type
 334 |     # Simple heuristic or check BioPython
 335 |     mol_type = "DNA"
 336 |     if HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 337 |         try:
 338 |              # Fast check first record using SeqIO.parse (stops lazy iterator after 1)
 339 |              # Avoids loading full alignment into memory
 340 |              # Fast heuristic check using first few records
 341 |              dna_votes = 0
 342 |              total_checked = 0
 343 |              
 344 |              for i, record in enumerate(SeqIO.parse(str(alignment_fasta), "fasta")):
 345 |                  if i >= 10: break # Check max 10 records
 346 |                  
 347 |                  seq = str(record.seq).upper().replace("-", "").replace("?", "")
 348 |                  if not seq: continue
 349 |                  
 350 |                  # Strong protein indicators: E, F, I, L, P, Q, Z
 351 |                  # If we see these, it is almost certainly Protein (or bad data, but assume Protein)
 352 |                  if any(c in seq for c in "EFILPQZ"):
 353 |                      # Found protein-specific char
 354 |                      total_checked += 1
 355 |                      continue # Counts as non-DNA (AA)
 356 |                      
 357 |                  # DNA heuristic: Count ACGTU + N
 358 |                  # Ambiguous DNA (R, Y, etc) exists, but high % of canonical bases implies DNA
 359 |                  dna_chars = sum(1 for c in seq if c in "ACGTUN")
 360 |                  if dna_chars / len(seq) > 0.85:
 361 |                      dna_votes += 1
 362 |                  total_checked += 1
 363 |                  
 364 |              # If majority of checked sequences look like AA, switch to AA.
 365 |              # (Default is DNA, so we only switch if evidence suggests AA)
 366 |              if total_checked > 0 and (dna_votes / total_checked) < 0.5:
 367 |                  mol_type = "AA"
 368 |                  
 369 |         except Exception:
 370 |             pass # Default DNA
 371 | 
 372 |     # 2. Validate & Resolve Parameters
 373 |     # We resolve again to sure we have the ResolvedRaxmlParams object and handle defaults robustly
 374 |     # Convert TreeBuilderParams to dict for validator
 375 |     params_dict = {
 376 |         "run_preset": params.run_preset,
 377 |         "bootstrap_preset": params.bootstrap_preset,
 378 |         "bootstrap_cap": params.bootstrap_cap,
 379 |         "enable_bootstrap": params.enable_bootstrap,
 380 |         "start_tree_override": params.start_tree_override,
 381 |         "moose_enabled": params.moose_enabled,
 382 |         "enable_early_stopping": params.early_stopping,
 383 |         "seed": params.seed,
 384 |         "outgroup": params.outgroup,
 385 |         "model": params.model
 386 |     }
 387 |     
 388 |     resolved = validate_and_resolve_raxml_params(params_dict, data_type=mol_type)
 389 |     
 390 |     # Check Support for Features
 391 |     has_moose_support = _check_raxml_feature(config, "--moose")
 392 |     has_early_stop_support = _check_raxml_feature(config, "--stop-rule")
 393 | 
 394 |     # 3. Handle MOOSE
 395 |     moose_mapping = None
 396 |     reduced_msa_path = None
 397 |     
 398 |     if resolved.enable_moose:
 399 |         if has_moose_support:
 400 |             sanitized_fasta_moose = output_newick.parent / "raxml_input_moose_sanitized.fasta"
 401 |             moose_mapping = sanitize_fasta_headers(alignment_fasta, sanitized_fasta_moose)
 402 |             
 403 |             best_model, best_msa = _run_moose(sanitized_fasta_moose, resolved, config, task_logger, job_id)
 404 |             if best_model:
 405 |                 resolved.model = best_model
 406 |             
 407 |             if best_msa != sanitized_fasta_moose and best_msa.exists():
 408 |                  reduced_msa_path = best_msa
 409 |                  # Note: reduced MSA retains simpler sanitized IDs, so moose_mapping is still valid for name restoration
 410 |                  
 411 |         else:
 412 |             task_logger.warning("MOOSE requested but not supported by installed RAxML-NG. Using default model.")
 413 |             
 414 |     # 4. Handle Early Stopping Flag
 415 |     if resolved.enable_early_stopping and not has_early_stop_support:
 416 |         task_logger.warning("Early stopping requested but not supported/found in help. Disabling.")
 417 |         resolved.enable_early_stopping = False
 418 | 
 419 |     # 5. Prepare Main Run
 420 |     prefix = str(output_newick.parent / "raxml_run")
 421 |     threads = _get_thread_count(params)
 422 |     
 423 |     if reduced_msa_path and moose_mapping:
 424 |          sanitized_fasta = reduced_msa_path
 425 |          name_mapping = moose_mapping
 426 |     else:
 427 |          sanitized_fasta = output_newick.parent / "raxml_input_sanitized.fasta"
 428 |          name_mapping = sanitize_fasta_headers(alignment_fasta, sanitized_fasta)
 429 |     
 430 |     cmd = _get_raxml_cmd(resolved, config, sanitized_fasta, prefix, threads)
 431 |     
 432 |     log_file = output_newick.parent.parent / "logs" / "tree_builder.log"
 433 |     
 434 |     if job_id:
 435 |         from app.workers.events import publish_command
 436 |         publish_command(job_id, "tree", cmd)
 437 |         
 438 |         exit_code, stats = run_command_streaming(
 439 |             cmd,
 440 |             stderr_path=log_file,
 441 |             on_stdout_line=_make_log_callback(job_id, "tree", "stdout"),
 442 |             on_stderr_line=_make_log_callback(job_id, "tree", "stderr"),
 443 |         )
 444 |         
 445 |         if exit_code != 0:
 446 |             raise RuntimeError(f"RAxML failed with exit code {exit_code}")
 447 |     else:
 448 |         returncode, stdout, stderr = run_command(cmd, log_file=log_file)
 449 |         if returncode != 0:
 450 |             raise RuntimeError(f"RAxML failed with return code {returncode}. See logs.")
 451 |             
 452 |     # 6. Output Handling
 453 |     best_tree = Path(f"{prefix}.raxml.bestTree")
 454 |     support_tree = Path(f"{prefix}.raxml.support")
 455 |     
 456 |     # Prefer supported tree if available (BS run), else best ML tree
 457 |     source_tree = support_tree if support_tree.exists() else best_tree
 458 |     
 459 |     if not source_tree.exists():
 460 |         raise RuntimeError("RAxML output tree not found.")
 461 |         
 462 |     # Copy to final internal location
 463 |     shutil.copy(source_tree, output_newick)
 464 |     
 465 |     # 7. Post-Processing: Outgroup Rerooting
 466 |     if resolved.outgroup and HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 467 |         try:
 468 |             task_logger.info(f"Rerooting tree on outgroup: {resolved.outgroup}")
 469 |             tree = Phylo.read(str(output_newick), "newick")
 470 |             
 471 |             # Find target (account for sanitization if needed, but resolved.outgroup should match original name)
 472 |             # But name_mapping keys are Original, Values are Sanitized.
 473 |             # RAxML output has Sanitized names.
 474 |             # resolved.outgroup is likely Original name (from UI).
 475 |             # So we need to look up sanitized name.
 476 |             target_name = resolved.outgroup
 477 |             
 478 |             # If name mapping used, find sanitized equivalent
 479 |             # Mapping: {SanitizedID: OriginalHeader}
 480 |             # We need to find the SanitizedID that corresponds to resolved.outgroup
 481 |             target_name = resolved.outgroup
 482 |             
 483 |             # Simple reverse lookup
 484 |             for safe_id, original_header in name_mapping.items():
 485 |                 if resolved.outgroup in original_header:
 486 |                      # Heuristic match: if selected outgroup name is substring of header
 487 |                      # This helps if outgroup param is "SeqA" but header is "SeqA SpeciesX"
 488 |                      target_name = safe_id
 489 |                      break
 490 |                 if original_header == resolved.outgroup:
 491 |                     target_name = safe_id
 492 |                     break
 493 |                 
 494 |             # Find clade
 495 |             target_clade = next((c for c in tree.find_clades() if c.name == target_name), None)
 496 |             
 497 |             if target_clade:
 498 |                 tree.root_with_outgroup(target_clade)
 499 |                 # Overwrite output_newick with rooted version
 500 |                 Phylo.write(tree, str(output_newick), "newick")
 501 |             else:
 502 |                 task_logger.warning(f"Outgroup {target_name} not found in tree. Skipping reroot.")
 503 |         except Exception as e:
 504 |             task_logger.error(f"Outgroup rerooting failed: {e}")
 505 | 
 506 |     # 8. Final Name Restoration & Nexus Conversion
 507 |     restore_tree_names(output_newick, name_mapping)
 508 |     _convert_newick_to_nexus(output_newick, output_nexus)
 509 | 
 510 | 
 511 | def _run_iqtree(
 512 |     alignment_fasta: Path,
 513 |     output_newick: Path,
 514 |     output_nexus: Path,
 515 |     params: TreeBuilderParams,
 516 |     config: Config,
 517 |     task_logger,
 518 |     job_id: Optional[str] = None
 519 | ):
 520 |     """Run IQ-TREE maximum likelihood tree inference."""
 521 |     prefix = str(output_newick.parent / "iqtree_run")
 522 |     threads = _get_thread_count(params)
 523 |     
 524 |     # Sanitize FASTA to create safe IDs
 525 |     sanitized_fasta = output_newick.parent / "iqtree_input_sanitized.fasta"
 526 |     name_mapping = sanitize_fasta_headers(alignment_fasta, sanitized_fasta)
 527 |     
 528 |     cmd = [
 529 |         config.IQTREE_BINARY,
 530 |         "-s", str(sanitized_fasta),
 531 |         "-m", params.model,
 532 |         "-nt", str(threads),
 533 |         "-pre", prefix,
 534 |         "-redo"
 535 |     ]
 536 |     
 537 |     if params.bootstrap and params.bootstrap > 0:
 538 |         cmd.extend(["-B", str(params.bootstrap)])
 539 |         
 540 |     log_file = output_newick.parent.parent / "logs" / "tree_builder.log"
 541 |     
 542 |     if job_id:
 543 |         # Publish command line (displayed in green)
 544 |         from app.workers.events import publish_command
 545 |         publish_command(job_id, "tree", cmd)
 546 |         
 547 |         exit_code, stats = run_command_streaming(
 548 |             cmd,
 549 |             stderr_path=log_file,
 550 |             on_stdout_line=_make_log_callback(job_id, "tree", "stdout"),  # IQ-TREE writes progress to stdout
 551 |             on_stderr_line=_make_log_callback(job_id, "tree", "stderr"),
 552 |         )
 553 |         
 554 |         if exit_code != 0:
 555 |             raise RuntimeError(f"IQ-TREE failed with exit code {exit_code}")
 556 |     else:
 557 |         returncode, stdout, stderr = run_command(cmd, log_file=log_file)
 558 |         
 559 |         if returncode != 0:
 560 |             raise RuntimeError(f"IQ-TREE failed with return code {returncode}. See logs.")
 561 |         
 562 |     # Output handling
 563 |     treefile = Path(f"{prefix}.treefile")
 564 |     contree = Path(f"{prefix}.contree")
 565 |     
 566 |     source_tree = contree if contree.exists() else treefile
 567 |     
 568 |     if source_tree.exists():
 569 |         shutil.copy(source_tree, output_newick)
 570 |         
 571 |         # Restore original names in the Newick tree
 572 |         restore_tree_names(output_newick, name_mapping)
 573 |         
 574 |         _convert_newick_to_nexus(output_newick, output_nexus)
 575 |     else:
 576 |         raise RuntimeError("IQ-TREE output tree not found.")
 577 | 
 578 | 
 579 | def _run_mrbayes(
 580 |     alignment_fasta: Path,
 581 |     output_newick: Path,
 582 |     output_nexus: Path,
 583 |     params: TreeBuilderParams,
 584 |     config: Config,
 585 |     task_logger,
 586 |     job_id: Optional[str] = None
 587 | ):
 588 |     """Run MrBayes Bayesian tree inference."""
 589 |     # Sanitize FASTA to create safe IDs before converting to NEXUS
 590 |     sanitized_fasta = output_newick.parent / "mrbayes_input_sanitized.fasta"
 591 |     name_mapping = sanitize_fasta_headers(alignment_fasta, sanitized_fasta)
 592 |     
 593 |     # MrBayes requires Nexus input with a block
 594 |     nexus_input = output_newick.parent / "mrbayes_input.nex"
 595 |     _convert_fasta_to_nexus(sanitized_fasta, nexus_input)
 596 |     
 597 |     # Append MrBayes block
 598 |     with open(nexus_input, "a") as f:
 599 |         f.write("\nbegin mrbayes;\n")
 600 |         f.write(f"   set autoclose=yes;\n")
 601 |         f.write(f"   lset nst=6 rates=gamma;\n")  # GTR+G equivalent
 602 |         f.write(f"   mcmc ngen={params.mcmc_generations} nchains={params.mcmc_nchains} nruns={params.mcmc_nruns} burninfrac=0.25;\n")
 603 |         f.write(f"   sump;\n")
 604 |         f.write(f"   sumt;\n")
 605 |         f.write("end;\n")
 606 |         
 607 |     cmd = [config.MRBAYES_BINARY, str(nexus_input)]
 608 |     
 609 |     log_file = output_newick.parent.parent / "logs" / "tree_builder.log"
 610 |     
 611 |     if job_id:
 612 |         # Publish command line (displayed in green)
 613 |         from app.workers.events import publish_command
 614 |         publish_command(job_id, "tree", cmd)
 615 |         
 616 |         # MrBayes prints progress to stdout
 617 |         exit_code, stats = run_command_streaming(
 618 |             cmd,
 619 |             stderr_path=log_file,
 620 |             on_stdout_line=_make_log_callback(job_id, "tree", "stdout"),  # MrBayes uses stdout
 621 |             on_stderr_line=_make_log_callback(job_id, "tree", "stderr"),
 622 |         )
 623 |         
 624 |         if exit_code != 0:
 625 |             raise RuntimeError(f"MrBayes failed with exit code {exit_code}")
 626 |     else:
 627 |         returncode, stdout, stderr = run_command(cmd, log_file=log_file)
 628 |         
 629 |         if returncode != 0:
 630 |             task_logger.error(f"MrBayes failed. RC={returncode}")
 631 |             task_logger.error(f"STDOUT: {stdout}")
 632 |             task_logger.error(f"STDERR: {stderr}")
 633 |             raise RuntimeError(f"MrBayes failed with return code {returncode}. Error: {stderr}")
 634 |         
 635 |     # Output: <input>.con.tre (Consensus tree)
 636 |     con_tree = Path(f"{nexus_input}.con.tre")
 637 |     
 638 |     if con_tree.exists():
 639 |         shutil.copy(con_tree, output_nexus)
 640 |         _convert_nexus_to_newick(output_nexus, output_newick)
 641 |         
 642 |         # Restore original names in output files
 643 |         restore_tree_names(output_newick, name_mapping)
 644 |         restore_tree_names(output_nexus, name_mapping)
 645 |     else:
 646 |         raise RuntimeError("MrBayes consensus tree not found.")
 647 | 
 648 | 
 649 | def _convert_newick_to_nexus(newick_path: Path, nexus_path: Path):
 650 |     """Convert Newick tree to NEXUS format."""
 651 |     if HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 652 |         try:
 653 |             tree = Phylo.read(str(newick_path), "newick")
 654 |             Phylo.write(tree, str(nexus_path), "nexus")
 655 |         except Exception:
 656 |             pass  # Best effort
 657 | 
 658 | 
 659 | def _convert_nexus_to_newick(nexus_path: Path, newick_path: Path):
 660 |     """
 661 |     Convert NEXUS tree to Newick format.
 662 |     
 663 |     Handles MrBayes annotations like [&prob=...] by extracting
 664 |     posterior probabilities as node labels.
 665 |     """
 666 |     if HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 667 |         try:
 668 |             import re
 669 |             
 670 |             # Helper definitions for robust matching
 671 |             _NUM_CORE = r"[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?(?:[eE][+\-]?[0-9]+)?"
 672 |             
 673 |             def _fmt_prob(s: str) -> str:
 674 |                 try:
 675 |                     x = float(s)
 676 |                     return f"{x:.3f}".rstrip("0").rstrip(".")
 677 |                 except (ValueError, TypeError):
 678 |                     return s
 679 | 
 680 |             content = nexus_path.read_text()
 681 |             
 682 |             # Case A: branch length comes first:  ): 0.05 [&prob=...]
 683 |             pat_a = re.compile(
 684 |                 rf"\)(?:\s*:\s*(?P<branch>{_NUM_CORE}))\s*\[[^\]]*?\bprob=\s*(?P<prob>{_NUM_CORE})\s*[^\]]*?\]",
 685 |                 re.IGNORECASE
 686 |             )
 687 | 
 688 |             # Case B: prob comes first:  )[&prob=...]:0.05
 689 |             pat_b = re.compile(
 690 |                 rf"\)\s*\[[^\]]*?\bprob=\s*(?P<prob>{_NUM_CORE})\s*[^\]]*?\](?:\s*:\s*(?P<branch>{_NUM_CORE}))?",
 691 |                 re.IGNORECASE
 692 |             )
 693 | 
 694 |             def repl(m: re.Match) -> str:
 695 |                 prob_val = _fmt_prob(m.group('prob'))
 696 |                 branch_val = m.group('branch')
 697 |                 branch_str = f":{branch_val}" if branch_val else ""
 698 |                 return f"){prob_val}{branch_str}"
 699 | 
 700 |             clean_content = pat_a.sub(repl, content)
 701 |             clean_content = pat_b.sub(repl, clean_content)
 702 | 
 703 |             # Remove any remaining [...] annotations
 704 |             clean_content = re.sub(r"\[[^\]]*\]", "", clean_content)
 705 |             
 706 |             from io import StringIO
 707 |             tree = Phylo.read(StringIO(clean_content), "nexus")
 708 |             Phylo.write(tree, str(newick_path), "newick")
 709 |         except Exception as e:
 710 |             logger.error(f"Failed to convert Nexus to Newick: {e}")
 711 |             raise
 712 | 
 713 | 
 714 | def _convert_fasta_to_nexus(fasta_path: Path, nexus_path: Path):
 715 |     """Convert aligned FASTA to NEXUS format for MrBayes."""
 716 |     if HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 717 |         # Read alignment
 718 |         aln = AlignIO.read(str(fasta_path), "fasta")
 719 |         
 720 |         # Detect molecule type
 721 |         if len(aln) > 0:
 722 |             seq_str = str(aln[0].seq).upper()
 723 |             dna_chars = set("ACGTN-")
 724 |             match_count = sum(1 for c in seq_str if c in dna_chars)
 725 |             is_dna = (match_count / len(seq_str)) > 0.8
 726 |             
 727 |             mol_type = "DNA" if is_dna else "protein"
 728 |             
 729 |             # Annotate records and sanitize IDs
 730 |             for record in aln:
 731 |                 record.annotations["molecule_type"] = mol_type
 732 |                 # Sanitize ID for MrBayes
 733 |                 clean_id = record.id.replace("|", "_").replace(":", "_").replace("-", "_").replace("'", "").replace(" ", "_")
 734 |                 clean_id = "".join(c for c in clean_id if c.isalnum() or c == "_")
 735 |                 record.id = clean_id
 736 |                 record.description = ""
 737 |                 
 738 |         AlignIO.write(aln, str(nexus_path), "nexus")
 739 |     else:
 740 |         raise RuntimeError("BioPython required for format conversion.")
 741 | 
 742 | 
 743 | def _run_fasttree(
 744 |     alignment_fasta: Path,
 745 |     output_newick: Path,
 746 |     output_nexus: Path,
 747 |     params: TreeBuilderParams,
 748 |     config: Config,
 749 |     task_logger,
 750 |     job_id: Optional[str] = None
 751 | ):
 752 |     """
 753 |     Run FastTree 2.2.0 tree inference.
 754 |     
 755 |     FastTree writes the tree to stdout.
 756 |     Command: fasttree -gtr -nt -gamma -boot 1000 alignment.fasta > tree.newick
 757 |     """
 758 |     
 759 |     # Check binary existence
 760 |     binary_path = Path(config.FASTTREE_BINARY)
 761 |     if not binary_path.exists() and not shutil.which(config.FASTTREE_BINARY):
 762 |         raise RuntimeError(f"FastTree binary not found at {config.FASTTREE_BINARY}")
 763 | 
 764 |     # Sanitize FASTA
 765 |     sanitized_fasta = output_newick.parent / "fasttree_input_sanitized.fasta"
 766 |     name_mapping = sanitize_fasta_headers(alignment_fasta, sanitized_fasta)
 767 |     
 768 |     # Build command
 769 |     cmd = [
 770 |         config.FASTTREE_BINARY,
 771 |         "-gtr",
 772 |         "-nt", 
 773 |         "-gamma",
 774 |         "-boot", str(1000) # Fixed 1000 bootstraps as per requirement
 775 |     ]
 776 |     
 777 |     # Check if alignment is valid (not empty)
 778 |     if sanitized_fasta.stat().st_size == 0:
 779 |          raise RuntimeError("Input alignment is empty.")
 780 | 
 781 |     cmd.append(str(sanitized_fasta))
 782 |     
 783 |     log_file = output_newick.parent.parent / "logs" / "tree_builder.log"
 784 |     
 785 |     task_logger.info(f"Running FastTree: {' '.join(cmd)}")
 786 |     
 787 |     if job_id:
 788 |         from app.workers.events import publish_command
 789 |         # We perform the redirect manually, but show it in the command event
 790 |         display_cmd = cmd + [">", str(output_newick)]
 791 |         publish_command(job_id, "tree", display_cmd)
 792 |         
 793 |         # FastTree writes progress to stderr, tree to stdout
 794 |         # stdout_path will automatically be opened by run_command_streaming
 795 |         exit_code, stats = run_command_streaming(
 796 |             cmd,
 797 |             stderr_path=log_file,
 798 |             stdout_path=output_newick, 
 799 |             on_stderr_line=_make_log_callback(job_id, "tree", "stderr")
 800 |         )
 801 |             
 802 |         if exit_code != 0:
 803 |              raise RuntimeError(f"FastTree failed with exit code {exit_code}")
 804 | 
 805 |     else:
 806 |         # specific handling if we assume run_command captures stdout
 807 |         # run_command returns (returncode, stdout, stderr)
 808 |         returncode, stdout, stderr = run_command(cmd, log_file=log_file)
 809 |         
 810 |         if returncode != 0:
 811 |             task_logger.error(f"FastTree failed. RC={returncode}")
 812 |             task_logger.error(f"STDERR: {stderr}")
 813 |             raise RuntimeError(f"FastTree failed with return code {returncode}. See logs.")
 814 |             
 815 |         # Write stdout to newick file
 816 |         with open(output_newick, "w") as f:
 817 |             f.write(stdout)
 818 | 
 819 |     # Check output
 820 |     if not output_newick.exists() or output_newick.stat().st_size == 0:
 821 |         raise RuntimeError("FastTree produced no output.")
 822 | 
 823 |     # Restore original names
 824 |     restore_tree_names(output_newick, name_mapping)
 825 |     
 826 |     # Convert to Nexus
 827 |     _convert_newick_to_nexus(output_newick, output_nexus)
 828 |

  20 | def load_tree_state(job_dir: Path) -> Dict:
  21 |     """
  22 |     Load tree_state.json if available.
  23 |     If not present, parse tree_original.newick or tree_original.nexus
  24 |     and build the initial tree state JSON.
  25 |     """
  26 |     state_path = job_dir / "tree_state.json"
  27 |     if state_path.exists():
  28 |         with open(state_path, "r") as f:
  29 |             return json.load(f)
  30 |             
  31 |     # Initialize from original tree
  32 |     newick_path = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_original.newick"
  33 |     if newick_path.exists():
  34 |         tree_json = parse_newick_to_json(newick_path)
  35 |         # Add metadata wrapper
  36 |         state = {
  37 |             "current_tree": "original",
  38 |             "tree_structure": tree_json,
  39 |             "pruned_taxa": [],
  40 |             "renames": {},
  41 |             "root": None # Default
  42 |         }
  43 |         
  44 |         # Determine rooting policy
  45 |         should_midpoint = True
  46 |         
  47 |         # Check if outgroup was specified in job params
  48 |         input_info_path = job_dir / "input_info.json"
  49 |         if input_info_path.exists():
  50 |             try:
  51 |                 with open(input_info_path, "r") as f:
  52 |                     info = json.load(f)
  53 |                     # Check metrics or raw params
  54 |                     # job params might be flattening, but 'outgroup' should be in tree_builder_params or metrics
  55 |                     # Let's check typical locations
  56 |                     metrics = info.get("metrics", {})
  57 |                     outgroup = info.get("outgroup") or metrics.get("outgroup")
  58 |                     
  59 |                     if outgroup:
  60 |                         should_midpoint = False
  61 |                         state["root"] = outgroup
  62 |                         state["root_mode"] = "OUTGROUP"
  63 |                         logging.info(f"Outgroup '{outgroup}' detected. Skipping default midpoint rooting.")
  64 |             except Exception as e:
  65 |                 logging.warning(f"Failed to check input_info for outgroup: {e}")
  66 | 
  67 |         # Default policy: Midpoint root the tree (if not outgroup rooted)
  68 |         if should_midpoint:
  69 |             try:
  70 |                 # Store original tree before midpoint rooting for toggle functionality
  71 |                 original_newick_path = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_original.newick"
  72 |                 if original_newick_path.exists():
  73 |                     with open(original_newick_path, "r") as f:
  74 |                         state["pre_midpoint_newick"] = f.read().strip()
  75 |                 
  76 |                 # We must pass the state as we just created it
  77 |                 state = midpoint_root(job_dir, state)
  78 |                 state["is_midpoint_rooted"] = True
  79 |                 logging.info("Applied default midpoint rooting.")
  80 |             except Exception as e:
  81 |                 state["is_midpoint_rooted"] = False
  82 |                 logging.warning(f"Default midpoint rooting skipped/failed: {e}")
  83 | 
  84 |         save_tree_state(job_dir, state)
  85 |         return state
  86 |         
  87 |     return {}

  90 |     """
  91 |     Save tree_json to tree_state.json with indentation.
  92 |     """

  94 |     with open(state_path, "w") as f:

  97 | def prune_taxa(job_dir: Path, tree_json: Dict, taxa_names: List[str]) -> Dict:
  98 |     """
  99 |     Remove one or more tips (or subtrees if internal nodes selected).
 100 |     Physically updates tree_pruned.newick and returns modified tree_json.
 101 |     """
 102 |     if not taxa_names:
 103 |         return tree_json
 104 | 
 105 |     if not HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 106 |         logger.error("BioPython not installed; cannot prune tree")
 107 |         raise RuntimeError("BioPython not installed; cannot prune tree")
 108 | 
 109 |     # Physical Pruning logic
 110 |     # Determine input path
 111 |     pruned_newick = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_pruned.newick"
 112 |     original_newick = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_original.newick"
 113 |     
 114 |     input_path = pruned_newick if pruned_newick.exists() else original_newick
 115 |     if not input_path.exists():
 116 |         raise FileNotFoundError("No tree file found to prune")
 117 | 
 118 |     try:
 119 |         tree = Phylo.read(str(input_path), "newick")
 120 |         
 121 |         # We need to prune multiple nodes. 
 122 |         targets = set(taxa_names)
 123 |         
 124 |         to_prune = []
 125 |         for clade in tree.find_clades():
 126 |             if clade.name in targets:
 127 |                 to_prune.append(clade)
 128 |                 
 129 |         if not to_prune:
 130 |              # Gather some available names for debugging
 131 |              available_tips = [t.name for t in tree.get_terminals() if t.name][:5]
 132 |              available_internal = [n.name for n in tree.get_nonterminals() if n.name][:5]
 133 |              msg = f"Targets {list(targets)[:3]}... not found in tree. " \
 134 |                    f"Sample tips: {available_tips}. Sample nodes: {available_internal}."
 135 |              logger.warning(msg)
 136 |              # Raise error to inform user in UI instead of silent failure
 137 |              raise ValueError(msg)
 138 |         
 139 |         # Only update metadata AFTER confirming targets exist in tree
 140 |         if "pruned_taxa" not in tree_json:
 141 |             tree_json["pruned_taxa"] = []
 142 |         
 143 |         for name in taxa_names:
 144 |             if name not in tree_json["pruned_taxa"]:
 145 |                 tree_json["pruned_taxa"].append(name)
 146 |              
 147 |         # Map parents for manual removal
 148 |         parents = {c: p for p in tree.find_clades() for c in p.clades}
 149 |         
 150 |         
 151 |         # Iterative Pruning with Cleanup
 152 |         # 1. Start with explicit targets
 153 |         queue = to_prune[:]
 154 |         processed = set()
 155 |         
 156 |         while queue:
 157 |             current = queue.pop(0)
 158 |             if current in processed:
 159 |                 continue
 160 |             processed.add(current)
 161 |             
 162 |             parent = parents.get(current)
 163 |             if not parent:
 164 |                 # Root - cannot prune? Or if distinct root node, maybe?
 165 |                 # Usually we don't prune root unless tree is empty
 166 |                 continue
 167 |                 
 168 |             # Remove from parent
 169 |             if current in parent.clades:
 170 |                 parent.clades.remove(current)
 171 |                 
 172 |             # Check status of parent
 173 |             # If parent now has 0 children, it has become a tip.
 174 |             # If parent was an original internal node (had children initially),
 175 |             # it is now an "artifact" tip. We should remove it too.
 176 |             # Exception: If parent is the Root, we might leave it or empty the tree.
 177 |             if len(parent.clades) == 0:
 178 |                  # It's empty. Is it the root?
 179 |                  if parent == tree.root:
 180 |                      # Attempt to leave empty root or handle gracefully
 181 |                      pass
 182 |                  else:
 183 |                      # Recursively prune this parent
 184 |                      queue.append(parent)
 185 |             
 186 |         # Post-pass: Collapse unifurcations (single-child internal nodes)
 187 |         # After pruning, internal nodes can end up with exactly one child,
 188 |         # creating visually odd "pass-through" nodes. We splice them out.
 189 |         _collapse_unifurcations(tree)
 190 | 
 191 |         # Save
 192 |         valid_path = job_dir / "tree"
 193 |         valid_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 194 |         Phylo.write(tree, str(valid_path / "tree_pruned.newick"), "newick")
 195 |         
 196 |         # Update JSON structure to match
 197 |         new_structure = _clade_to_json(tree.root)
 198 |         tree_json["tree_structure"] = new_structure
 199 |         
 200 |         renames = tree_json.get("renames", {})
 201 |         apply_state_to_structure(new_structure, renames, set()) 
 202 |         
 203 |         return tree_json
 204 |         
 205 |     except Exception as e:
 206 |         logger.error(f"Prune failed: {e}")
 207 |         raise
 208 | 
 209 | def rename_tip(tree_json: Dict, old_name: str, new_name: str) -> Dict:
 210 |     """
 211 |     Change display_name of a tip. Preserve original_name.
 212 |     """
 213 |     if "renames" not in tree_json:
 214 |         tree_json["renames"] = {}
 215 |         
 216 |     tree_json["renames"][old_name] = new_name
 217 |     
 218 |     def apply_rename(node):
 219 |         if node.get("name") == old_name or node.get("original_name") == old_name:
 220 |             node["display_name"] = new_name
 221 |             # We keep 'name' as identifier usually, but display_name for UI
 222 |             return True
 223 |         if "children" in node:
 224 |             for child in node["children"]:
 225 |                 apply_rename(child)
 226 |         return False
 227 | 
 228 |     if "tree_structure" in tree_json:
 229 |         apply_rename(tree_json["tree_structure"])
 230 |         
 231 |     return tree_json
 232 | 
 233 | def apply_state_to_structure(node: Dict, renames: Dict, pruned_taxa: Set[str]):
 234 |     """
 235 |     Recursively apply metadata (renames, prune status) to the tree structure.
 236 |     """
 237 |     original_name = node.get("original_name")
 238 |     if original_name:
 239 |         # Apply Rename
 240 |         if original_name in renames:
 241 |             node["display_name"] = renames[original_name]
 242 |         
 243 |         # Apply Prune
 244 |         if original_name in pruned_taxa:
 245 |             node["pruned"] = True
 246 |             
 247 |     if "children" in node:
 248 |         for child in node["children"]:
 249 |             apply_state_to_structure(child, renames, pruned_taxa)
 250 | 
 251 | def reroot_tree(job_dir: Path, tree_json: Dict, root_target: str) -> Dict:
 252 |     """
 253 |     Reroot using any valid node (tip or internal). 
 254 |     Update tree_json's root and save the modified tree to tree_pruned.newick.
 255 |     """
 256 |     if not HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 257 |         return tree_json # Return unchanged if no library
 258 | 
 259 |     # Determine input path: prefer existing modified tree
 260 |     pruned_newick = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_pruned.newick"
 261 |     original_newick = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_original.newick"
 262 |     
 263 |     input_path = pruned_newick if pruned_newick.exists() else original_newick
 264 |     if not input_path.exists():
 265 |         raise FileNotFoundError("No tree file found to reroot")
 266 | 
 267 |     try:
 268 |         # Load the tree
 269 |         tree = Phylo.read(str(input_path), "newick")
 270 |         
 271 |         # Validate target
 272 |         if not root_target:
 273 |              raise ValueError("Reroot target cannot be empty")
 274 |              
 275 |         # Find the target clade (search ANY clade, not just terminals)
 276 |         target_clade = None
 277 |         for clade in tree.find_clades():
 278 |             if clade.name == root_target:
 279 |                 target_clade = clade
 280 |                 break
 281 |         
 282 |         if target_clade is None:
 283 |              # Fallback: check confidence converted to string
 284 |              # This handles cases where Newick I/O converted numeric name -> confidence
 285 |              for clade in tree.find_clades():
 286 |                  if clade.confidence is not None and str(clade.confidence) == root_target:
 287 |                      target_clade = clade
 288 |                      break
 289 | 
 290 |         if target_clade is None:
 291 |              # Useful debug info in the error
 292 |              internal = [c.name for c in tree.get_nonterminals() if c.name][:15]
 293 |              tips = [c.name for c in tree.get_terminals() if c.name][:15]
 294 |              raise ValueError(
 295 |                 f"Root target not found: {root_target}. "
 296 |                 f"Example internal names: {internal}. Example tip names: {tips}"
 297 |              )
 298 |         
 299 |         # Reroot
 300 |         tree.root_with_outgroup(target_clade)
 301 |         
 302 |         # Ladderize (Deterministic)
 303 |         ladderize_tree(tree)
 304 | 
 305 |         # FIX: Ensure confidence is dropped for named nodes before saving/returning
 306 |         _drop_confidence_when_named(tree)
 307 | 
 308 |         # Build structure
 309 |         new_structure = _clade_to_json(tree.root)
 310 |         
 311 |         # Update state
 312 |         tree_json["root"] = root_target
 313 |         tree_json["root_mode"] = "TIP"
 314 |         tree_json["root_target"] = root_target
 315 |         tree_json["current_tree"] = "pruned"
 316 |         tree_json["tree_structure"] = new_structure
 317 |         
 318 |         # Re-apply metadata
 319 |         renames = tree_json.get("renames", {})
 320 |         pruned_taxa = set(tree_json.get("pruned_taxa", []))
 321 |         apply_state_to_structure(new_structure, renames, pruned_taxa)
 322 |         
 323 |         # Save physical file
 324 |         valid_path = job_dir / "tree"
 325 |         valid_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 326 |         Phylo.write(tree, str(valid_path / "tree_pruned.newick"), "newick")
 327 |         logging.info(f"Successfully wrote rerooted tree to {valid_path / 'tree_pruned.newick'}")
 328 |         
 329 |         return tree_json
 330 |         
 331 |     except Exception as e:
 332 |         logger.error(f"Reroot failed: {e}")
 333 |         raise
 334 | 
 335 |     return tree_json
 336 | 
 337 | def midpoint_root(job_dir: Path, tree_json: Dict) -> Dict:
 338 |     """
 339 |     Reroot tree at midpoint.
 340 |     Requires branch lengths.
 341 |     """
 342 |     if not HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 343 |         raise RuntimeError("Biopython not installed; midpoint rooting unavailable")
 344 | 
 345 |     # Determine input path
 346 |     pruned_newick = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_pruned.newick"
 347 |     original_newick = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_original.newick"
 348 |     
 349 |     input_path = pruned_newick if pruned_newick.exists() else original_newick
 350 |     if not input_path.exists():
 351 |         raise FileNotFoundError("No tree file found to reroot")
 352 | 
 353 |     try:
 354 |         tree = Phylo.read(str(input_path), "newick")
 355 |         
 356 |         # Check for branch lengths
 357 |         # Heuristic: Verify we have at least one positive branch length.
 358 |         has_lengths = any((c.branch_length or 0) > 0 for c in tree.find_clades())
 359 |         if not has_lengths:
 360 |              raise ValueError("Cannot perform midpoint rooting: Tree has no valid branch lengths.")
 361 | 
 362 |         # Attempt BioPython midpoint rooting
 363 |         # This modifies the tree in-place usually, but sometimes returns new tree depending on version.
 364 |         # Phylo.NewickIO check: root_at_midpoint modifies in place.
 365 |         try:
 366 |             tree.root_at_midpoint()
 367 |         except Exception as e:
 368 |             logger.warning(f"Midpoint rooting failed (Math domain or topology?): {e}")
 369 |             raise ValueError(f"Midpoint rooting failed: {e}") from e
 370 | 
 371 |         # Ladderize (Deterministic)
 372 |         ladderize_tree(tree)
 373 | 
 374 |         # Ensure unique labels for stable IDs
 375 |         ensure_unique_labels(tree)
 376 | 
 377 |         # FIX: Ensure confidence is dropped for named nodes before saving/returning
 378 |         _drop_confidence_when_named(tree)
 379 | 
 380 |         # Build structure
 381 |         new_structure = _clade_to_json(tree.root)
 382 |         
 383 |         # Update state
 384 |         tree_json["root"] = None
 385 |         tree_json["root_mode"] = "MIDPOINT"
 386 |         tree_json["root_target"] = None
 387 |         tree_json["current_tree"] = "pruned"
 388 |         tree_json["tree_structure"] = new_structure
 389 |         tree_json["is_midpoint_rooted"] = True
 390 |         
 391 |         # Re-apply metadata
 392 |         renames = tree_json.get("renames", {})
 393 |         pruned_taxa = set(tree_json.get("pruned_taxa", []))
 394 |         apply_state_to_structure(new_structure, renames, pruned_taxa)
 395 |         
 396 |         # Save physical file
 397 |         valid_path = job_dir / "tree"
 398 |         valid_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 399 |         Phylo.write(tree, str(valid_path / "tree_pruned.newick"), "newick")
 400 |         
 401 |         return tree_json
 402 |         
 403 |     except Exception as e:
 404 |         logger.error(f"Midpoint root failed: {e}")
 405 |         raise
 406 | 
 407 | 
 408 | def undo_midpoint_root(job_dir: Path, tree_json: Dict) -> Dict:
 409 |     """
 410 |     Restore tree to pre-midpoint rooted state.
 411 |     Uses the stored pre_midpoint_newick from tree state.
 412 |     """
 413 |     if not HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 414 |         raise RuntimeError("Biopython not installed; cannot restore tree")
 415 | 
 416 |     pre_midpoint_newick = tree_json.get("pre_midpoint_newick")
 417 |     if not pre_midpoint_newick:
 418 |         raise ValueError("No pre-midpoint tree backup found. Cannot undo midpoint rooting.")
 419 | 
 420 |     try:
 421 |         # Parse the stored original newick
 422 |         tree = Phylo.read(StringIO(pre_midpoint_newick), "newick")
 423 |         
 424 |         # Ensure unique labels
 425 |         ensure_unique_labels(tree)
 426 |         
 427 |         # Ladderize for consistent display
 428 |         ladderize_tree(tree)
 429 |         
 430 |         # FIX: Ensure confidence is dropped for named nodes
 431 |         _drop_confidence_when_named(tree)
 432 | 
 433 |         # Build structure
 434 |         new_structure = _clade_to_json(tree.root)
 435 |         
 436 |         # Update state
 437 |         tree_json["root"] = None
 438 |         tree_json["root_mode"] = "ORIGINAL"
 439 |         tree_json["root_target"] = None
 440 |         tree_json["current_tree"] = "pruned"
 441 |         tree_json["tree_structure"] = new_structure
 442 |         tree_json["is_midpoint_rooted"] = False
 443 |         
 444 |         # Re-apply metadata
 445 |         renames = tree_json.get("renames", {})
 446 |         pruned_taxa = set(tree_json.get("pruned_taxa", []))
 447 |         apply_state_to_structure(new_structure, renames, pruned_taxa)
 448 |         
 449 |         # Save physical file
 450 |         valid_path = job_dir / "tree"
 451 |         valid_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 452 |         Phylo.write(tree, str(valid_path / "tree_pruned.newick"), "newick")
 453 |         
 454 |         return tree_json
 455 |         
 456 |     except Exception as e:
 457 |         logger.error(f"Undo midpoint root failed: {e}")
 458 |         raise
 459 | 
 460 | def extract_pruned_fasta(original_fasta: Path, tree_json: Dict, output_fasta: Path) -> None:
 461 |     """
 462 |     Write a new FASTA file containing only sequences corresponding to non-pruned tips.
 463 |     Use original_name to map FASTA headers.
 464 |     """
 465 |     pruned_taxa = set(tree_json.get("pruned_taxa", []))
 466 |     
 467 |     if not HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 468 |         raise RuntimeError("BioPython required for FASTA manipulation")
 469 |         
 470 |     sequences = []
 471 |     with open(original_fasta, "r") as f:
 472 |         for record in SeqIO.parse(f, "fasta"):
 473 |             if record.id not in pruned_taxa:
 474 |                 sequences.append(record)
 475 |                 
 476 |     with open(output_fasta, "w") as f:
 477 |         SeqIO.write(sequences, f, "fasta")
 478 | 
 479 | def recompute_tree(job_dir: Path, job_params: JobParams, config: Config, logger) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 480 |     """
 481 |     Re-run the alignment, trimming, and tree inference pipeline 
 482 |     using only sequences present in the pruned tree.
 483 |     """
 484 |     logger.info("Starting tree recomputation...")
 485 |     
 486 |     # 1. Load state
 487 |     tree_json = load_tree_state(job_dir)
 488 |     
 489 |     # 2. Extract pruned FASTA
 490 |     # We need the original input (unaligned)
 491 |     input_raw = job_dir / "input" / "input_raw.fasta"
 492 |     alignment_pruned_path = job_dir / "alignment" / "alignment_pruned.fasta" # Unaligned input for re-run
 493 |     
 494 |     extract_pruned_fasta(input_raw, tree_json, alignment_pruned_path)
 495 |     
 496 |     # 3. Re-align
 497 |     # We need alignment params. Assuming they are in job_params or we use defaults.
 498 |     # If job_params passed to this function has them, great.
 499 |     # But wait, job_params usually comes from the initial job submission.
 500 |     # The recompute endpoint might pass updated params? 
 501 |     # For now, let's reuse original params or defaults.
 502 |     
 503 |     align_params = job_params.alignment_params or AlignmentParams(method="default")
 504 |     alignment_pruned_aligned_path = job_dir / "alignment" / "alignment_pruned_aligned.fasta" # Result of alignment
 505 |     
 506 |     from app.services.alignment_service import run_alignment
 507 |     run_alignment(alignment_pruned_path, alignment_pruned_aligned_path, align_params, config, logger)
 508 |     
 509 |     # 4. Re-trim
 510 |     trim_params = job_params.trimming_params or TrimmingParams(method="none")
 511 |     alignment_pruned_trimmed_path = job_dir / "alignment" / "alignment_pruned_trimmed.fasta"
 512 |     
 513 |     from app.services.trimming_service import run_trimming
 514 |     run_trimming(alignment_pruned_aligned_path, alignment_pruned_trimmed_path, trim_params.method, config, logger)
 515 |     
 516 |     # 5. Re-build Tree
 517 |     tree_params = job_params.tree_builder_params or TreeBuilderParams(method="nj")
 518 |     tree_pruned_newick = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_pruned.newick"
 519 |     tree_pruned_nexus = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_pruned.nexus"
 520 |     
 521 |     from app.services.tree_builder_service import run_tree_builder
 522 |     metadata = run_tree_builder(
 523 |         alignment_pruned_trimmed_path,
 524 |         tree_pruned_newick,
 525 |         tree_pruned_nexus,
 526 |         tree_params,
 527 |         config,
 528 |         logger
 529 |     )
 530 |     
 531 |     # 6. Update State
 532 |     tree_json["current_tree"] = "pruned"
 533 |     # We might want to update the tree structure in JSON too?
 534 |     # Yes, parse the new tree
 535 |     new_structure = parse_newick_to_json(tree_pruned_newick)
 536 |     tree_json["tree_structure"] = new_structure
 537 |     save_tree_state(job_dir, tree_json)
 538 |     
 539 |     # Save metadata
 540 |     with open(job_dir / "tree" / "tree_pruned_metadata.json", "w") as f:
 541 |         json.dump(metadata, f, indent=2)
 542 |         
 543 |     return {
 544 |         "status": "completed",
 545 |         "newick": str(tree_pruned_newick),
 546 |         "nexus": str(tree_pruned_nexus),
 547 |         "metadata": metadata
 548 |     }
 549 | 
 550 | def parse_newick_to_json(path: Path) -> Dict:
 551 |     """
 552 |     Convert a Newick tree to our JSON format.
 553 |     """
 554 |     if not HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 555 |         return {"error": "BioPython missing"}
 556 |         
 557 |     try:
 558 |         tree = Phylo.read(str(path), "newick")
 559 |         return _clade_to_json(tree.root)
 560 |     except Exception as e:
 561 |         logger.error(f"Failed to parse Newick: {e}")
 562 |         return {}
 563 | 
 564 | def _clade_to_json(clade):
 565 |     node = {
 566 |         "name": clade.name,
 567 |         "original_name": clade.name,
 568 |         "branch_length": clade.branch_length,
 569 |         "confidence": clade.confidence if clade.confidence is not None else getattr(clade, '_poly_confidence', None)
 570 |     }
 571 |     if clade.clades:
 572 |         node["children"] = [_clade_to_json(c) for c in clade.clades]
 573 |     return node
 574 | 
 575 | 
 576 | def _collapse_unifurcations(tree) -> None:
 577 |     """
 578 |     Collapse internal nodes that have exactly one child (unifurcations).
 579 |     
 580 |     After pruning, you can end up with internal nodes that only have one child,
 581 |     which is structurally redundant and looks odd in visualizations.
 582 |     This function splices out such nodes by promoting the child to replace
 583 |     the single-child parent.
 584 |     
 585 |     Branch lengths are summed when collapsing: if parent has length 0.1 and
 586 |     child has length 0.2, the surviving child gets length 0.3.
 587 |     
 588 |     Modifies tree in-place.
 589 |     """
 590 |     # Handle root unifurcation first
 591 |     # If root has exactly one child, promote that child to be the new root
 592 |     while tree.root.clades and len(tree.root.clades) == 1:
 593 |         only_child = tree.root.clades[0]
 594 |         # Sum branch lengths (root typically has no length, but be safe)
 595 |         if tree.root.branch_length and only_child.branch_length:
 596 |             only_child.branch_length += tree.root.branch_length
 597 |         elif tree.root.branch_length:
 598 |             only_child.branch_length = tree.root.branch_length
 599 |         # Promote child to root
 600 |         tree.root = only_child
 601 |         # Continue loop in case the new root is also a unifurcation
 602 |     
 603 |     # Build parent map for non-root collapses
 604 |     parents = {c: p for p in tree.find_clades() for c in p.clades}
 605 |     
 606 |     # Iteratively collapse until no unifurcations remain
 607 |     # We need to iterate because collapsing one node might create another
 608 |     changed = True
 609 |     while changed:
 610 |         changed = False
 611 |         # Get fresh list of non-terminals each iteration
 612 |         for clade in list(tree.get_nonterminals()):
 613 |             # Skip if it's the root or doesn't have exactly one child
 614 |             if clade == tree.root or len(clade.clades) != 1:
 615 |                 continue
 616 |                 
 617 |             only_child = clade.clades[0]
 618 |             parent = parents.get(clade)
 619 |             
 620 |             if parent is None:
 621 |                 # This shouldn't happen if we handled root above, but be safe
 622 |                 continue
 623 |             
 624 |             # Sum branch lengths
 625 |             new_length = only_child.branch_length
 626 |             if clade.branch_length is not None:
 627 |                 if new_length is not None:
 628 |                     new_length += clade.branch_length
 629 |                 else:
 630 |                     new_length = clade.branch_length
 631 |             only_child.branch_length = new_length
 632 |             
 633 |             # Replace clade with only_child in parent's children list
 634 |             idx = parent.clades.index(clade)
 635 |             parent.clades[idx] = only_child
 636 |             
 637 |             # Update parent map for the promoted child
 638 |             parents[only_child] = parent
 639 |             
 640 |             changed = True
 641 |             # Rebuild parent map since tree structure changed
 642 |             parents = {c: p for p in tree.find_clades() for c in p.clades}
 643 |             break  # Restart iteration with fresh non-terminal list
 644 | 
 645 | 
 646 | def _drop_confidence_when_named(tree) -> None:
 647 |     """
 648 |     Biopython Newick writer concatenates clade.name + clade.confidence.
 649 |     If we set name (e.g., Node_12_100), confidence must be None to prevent 
 650 |     outputting Node_12_100100.
 651 |     """
 652 |     for clade in tree.get_nonterminals():
 653 |         if clade.name and clade.confidence is not None:
 654 |             # Stash confidence so it's not lost when we clear it for Newick write safety
 655 |             clade._poly_confidence = clade.confidence
 656 |             clade.confidence = None
 657 | 
 658 | def ensure_unique_labels(tree) -> bool:
 659 |     """
 660 |     Traverse the tree and ensure every internal node has a unique name.
 661 |     Tips are PROTECTED: Duplicate tip names will raise ValueError.
 662 |     Internal nodes with no name or duplicate/numeric names will be assigned 'Node_{i}'.
 663 |     Returns True if changes were made.
 664 |     """
 665 |     seen_names = set()
 666 |     changes_made = False
 667 |     counter = 1
 668 |     
 669 |     # Pass 1: Collect tip names and validate uniqueness
 670 |     for clade in tree.get_terminals():
 671 |         if not clade.name:
 672 |              # Should practically never happen for a valid Newick tip, but strict check
 673 |              raise ValueError("Tip node missing name")
 674 |         if clade.name in seen_names:
 675 |              raise ValueError(f"Duplicate tip name found: '{clade.name}'. Tips must be unique.")
 676 |         seen_names.add(clade.name)
 677 |         
 678 |     # Pass 2: Handle Internal Nodes
 679 |     # We want to preserve existing unique non-numeric internal names if possible
 680 |     
 681 |     # Collect existing internal names to avoid collisions
 682 |     existing_internal_names = set()
 683 |     for clade in tree.get_nonterminals():
 684 |         if clade.name:
 685 |             existing_internal_names.add(clade.name)
 686 | 
 687 |     # Assign/Sanitize
 688 |     seen_in_pass = set()
 689 |     import re
 690 |     numeric_pattern = re.compile(r'^\d+(\.\d+)?$')
 691 | 
 692 |     for clade in tree.get_nonterminals():
 693 |         needs_rename = False
 694 |         
 695 |         # Check original numeric value (Name or Confidence)
 696 |         original_numeric_match = None
 697 |         is_numeric_name = False
 698 |         
 699 |         if clade.name:
 700 |             if numeric_pattern.match(clade.name):
 701 |                 is_numeric_name = True
 702 |                 # Filter out likely IDs (e.g. 6100)
 703 |                 try:
 704 |                     val = float(clade.name)
 705 |                     if val <= 100:
 706 |                         original_numeric_match = clade.name
 707 |                 except ValueError:
 708 |                     pass
 709 |         elif clade.confidence is not None:
 710 |              # Check confidence if name missing
 711 |              conf_str = str(clade.confidence)
 712 |              if numeric_pattern.match(conf_str):
 713 |                  # Filter out likely IDs
 714 |                  try:
 715 |                     val = float(conf_str)
 716 |                     if val <= 100:
 717 |                         original_numeric_match = conf_str
 718 |                  except ValueError:
 719 |                     pass
 720 | 
 721 |         # Criteria for renaming internal node:
 722 |         # 1. Empty name
 723 |         # 2. Duplicate of matched Tip (cannot clash with tips)
 724 |         # 3. Duplicate of already seen internal name
 725 |         # 4. Numeric name (matches regex) - sanitize to avoid confusion
 726 |         
 727 |         if not clade.name:
 728 |             needs_rename = True
 729 |         elif clade.name in seen_names: # Clash with tip
 730 |             needs_rename = True
 731 |         elif clade.name in seen_in_pass: # Clash with other internal
 732 |             needs_rename = True
 733 |         elif is_numeric_name: # Numeric safety
 734 |             needs_rename = True
 735 |             
 736 |         if needs_rename:
 737 |             changes_made = True
 738 |             # Generate unique name
 739 |             while True:
 740 |                 candidate = f"Node_{counter}"
 741 |                 
 742 |                 # Hybrid Logic: If we are renaming, preserve value if we found one
 743 |                 if original_numeric_match:
 744 |                     candidate = f"{candidate}_{original_numeric_match}"
 745 |                     
 746 |                 counter += 1
 747 |                 
 748 |                 # Must be unique globally (not in Tips, not in Existing Internal, not in Newly Assigned)
 749 |                 if (candidate not in seen_names and 
 750 |                     candidate not in existing_internal_names and 
 751 |                     candidate not in seen_in_pass):
 752 |                     clade.name = candidate
 753 |                     # Add to "existing" so we don't re-generate it
 754 |                     existing_internal_names.add(candidate)
 755 |                     
 756 |                     # Probably not needed, but just in case
 757 |                     if clade.confidence is not None:
 758 |                          clade._poly_confidence = clade.confidence
 759 |                     clade.confidence = None
 760 |                     break
 761 |         
 762 |         if clade.name:
 763 |             seen_in_pass.add(clade.name)
 764 |             
 765 |     # CRITICAL FIX: Prevent BioPython from doubling up (Name + Confidence)
 766 |     # This applies to ALL named internal nodes, whether we renamed them or they came named.
 767 |     _drop_confidence_when_named(tree)
 768 |     
 769 |     return changes_made
 770 | 
 771 | def ladderize_tree(tree, ascending: bool = False):
 772 |     """
 773 |     Sorts clades in-place to ensure deterministic visual output.
 774 |     Sort keys:
 775 |       1. Number of descendant tips (size).
 776 |       2. Lexicographically smallest tip name (min_leaf_name).
 777 |     """
 778 |     
 779 |     # Pre-calculate metrics to avoid re-traversing constantly
 780 |     # We'll attach temp attributes to nodes
 781 |     
 782 |     def get_metrics(clade):
 783 |         if hasattr(clade, "_ladder_metrics"):
 784 |             return clade._ladder_metrics
 785 |             
 786 |         if clade.is_terminal():
 787 |             count = 1
 788 |             min_name = clade.name or ""
 789 |         else:
 790 |             count = 0
 791 |             min_name = None
 792 |             for c in clade.clades:
 793 |                 c_metrics = get_metrics(c)
 794 |                 count += c_metrics[0]
 795 |                 c_min = c_metrics[1]
 796 |                 if min_name is None or (c_min is not None and c_min < min_name):
 797 |                     min_name = c_min
 798 |                     
 799 |             if min_name is None: min_name = "" # Should not happen if tips named
 800 |             
 801 |         clade._ladder_metrics = (count, min_name)
 802 |         return (count, min_name)
 803 | 
 804 |     # Calculate all metrics from root
 805 |     get_metrics(tree.root)
 806 |     
 807 |     # Sort recursively
 808 |     def sort_clade(clade):
 809 |         if not clade.is_terminal():
 810 |             # Sort children
 811 |             # Python's sort is stable.
 812 |             # We want primary: count, secondary: min_name
 813 |             # If ascending=True: Small counts first.
 814 |             
 815 |             clade.clades.sort(key=lambda c: (c._ladder_metrics[0], c._ladder_metrics[1]), reverse=not ascending)
 816 |             
 817 |             for c in clade.clades:
 818 |                 sort_clade(c)
 819 | 
 820 |     sort_clade(tree.root)
 821 |     
 822 |     # Cleanup
 823 |     for clade in tree.find_clades():
 824 |         if hasattr(clade, "_ladder_metrics"):
 825 |             del clade._ladder_metrics
 826 | 
 827 | def initialize_tree(job_dir: Path) -> Path:
 828 |     """
 829 |     Ensure tree_pruned.newick exists and has unique node labels.
 830 |     If missing, copy original -> pruned, sanitize labels, and save.
 831 |     Returns path to pruned tree.
 832 |     """
 833 |     pruned_path = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_pruned.newick"
 834 |     original_path = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_original.newick"
 835 |     
 836 |     if pruned_path.exists():
 837 |         return pruned_path
 838 |         
 839 |     if not original_path.exists():
 840 |         raise FileNotFoundError("Original tree not found")
 841 |         
 842 |     if not HAS_BIOPYTHON:
 843 |         # Just copy if we can't sanitize
 844 |         shutil.copy(original_path, pruned_path)
 845 |         return pruned_path
 846 |         
 847 |     try:
 848 |         tree = Phylo.read(str(original_path), "newick")
 849 |         ensure_unique_labels(tree)
 850 |         
 851 |         # Save to pruned
 852 |         Phylo.write(tree, str(pruned_path), "newick")
 853 |         
 854 |         # Initialize basic state JSON too
 855 |         tree_json = _clade_to_json(tree.root)
 856 |         state = {
 857 |             "current_tree": "pruned",
 858 |             "tree_structure": tree_json,
 859 |             "pruned_taxa": [],
 860 |             "renames": {},
 861 |             "root": None
 862 |         }
 863 |         save_tree_state(job_dir, state)
 864 |         
 865 |     except Exception as e:
 866 |         logger.error(f"Failed to initialize tree: {e}")
 867 |         # Fallback copy
 868 |         shutil.copy(original_path, pruned_path)
 869 |         
 870 |     return pruned_path
 871 | 
 872 | def json_to_newick(tree_json: Dict) -> str:
 873 |     """
 874 |     Serialize JSON tree back to Newick.
 875 |     """
 876 |     # If tree_json has "tree_structure", use that
 877 |     node = tree_json.get("tree_structure", tree_json)
 878 |     
 879 |     def _node_to_newick(n):
 880 |         name = n.get("name", "")
 881 |         length = n.get("branch_length")
 882 |         length_str = f":{length}" if length is not None else ""
 883 |         
 884 |         if "children" in n and n["children"]:
 885 |             children_str = ",".join([_node_to_newick(c) for c in n["children"]])
 886 |             return f"({children_str}){name}{length_str}"
 887 |         else:
 888 |             return f"{name}{length_str}"
 889 | 
 890 |     return _node_to_newick(node) + ";"

FILE: app/services/trimming_service.py
