   1 | """
   2 | Trimming service module.
   3 | 
   4 | Provides functions for trimming multiple sequence alignments:
   5 | - trimAl
   6 | - BMGE
   7 | 
   8 | When job_id is provided, streams log output to Redis for real-time SSE updates.
   9 | """
  10 | 
  11 | import shutil
  12 | from pathlib import Path
  13 | from typing import Optional
  14 | 
  15 | from app.config import Config
  16 | from app.services.subprocess_utils import run_command, run_command_streaming
  17 | 
  18 | 
  19 | def run_trimming(
  20 |     input_alignment: Path,
  21 |     output_alignment: Path,
  22 |     trim_method: str,
  23 |     config: Config,
  24 |     logger,
  25 |     job_id: Optional[str] = None
  26 | ) -> None:
  27 |     """
  28 |     Apply gap trimming to an alignment.
  29 |     
  30 |     trim_method options:
  31 |         - "none" - No trimming (copy input to output)
  32 |         - "trimal" - trimAl default settings
  33 |         - "bmge" - BMGE default settings
  34 |     
  35 |     Args:
  36 |         input_alignment: Path to input aligned FASTA
  37 |         output_alignment: Path for output trimmed FASTA
  38 |         trim_method: Trimming method name
  39 |         config: Application config
  40 |         logger: Logger instance
  41 |         job_id: Optional job ID for real-time event streaming
  42 |     """
  43 |     method = trim_method.lower()
  44 |     
  45 |     logger.info(f"Starting trimming with method: {method}")
  46 |     
  47 |     try:
  48 |         if method == "none" or not method:
  49 |             shutil.copy(input_alignment, output_alignment)
  50 |             logger.info("Trimming skipped (method='none'). Copied input to output.")
  51 |             return
  52 | 
  53 |         if method == "trimal":
  54 |             _run_trimal(input_alignment, output_alignment, config, logger, job_id)
  55 |         elif method == "bmge":
  56 |             _run_bmge(input_alignment, output_alignment, config, logger, job_id)
  57 |         else:
  58 |             # Fallback to none if unknown, or raise? 
  59 |             # Let's raise to be strict.
  60 |             raise ValueError(f"Unsupported trimming method: {method}")
  61 | 
  62 |         if not output_alignment.exists() or output_alignment.stat().st_size == 0:
  63 |              raise RuntimeError(f"Trimming failed: Output file {output_alignment} is missing or empty.")
  64 | 
  65 |         logger.info(f"Trimming completed successfully. Output: {output_alignment}")
  66 | 
  67 |     except Exception as e:
  68 |         logger.error(f"Trimming failed: {e}")
  69 |         raise
  70 | 
  71 | 
  72 | def _make_log_callback(job_id: Optional[str], step: str, stream: str):
  73 |     """Create a callback function for streaming output to Redis."""
  74 |     if not job_id:
  75 |         return None
  76 |     
  77 |     from app.workers.events import publish_log
  78 |     
  79 |     def callback(line: str):
  80 |         publish_log(job_id, step, stream, line)
  81 |     
  82 |     return callback
  83 | 
  84 | 
  85 | def _run_trimal(
  86 |     input_alignment: Path,
  87 |     output_alignment: Path,
  88 |     config: Config,
  89 |     logger,
  90 |     job_id: Optional[str] = None
  91 | ):
  92 |     """Run trimAl alignment trimming."""
  93 |     cmd = [
  94 |         config.TRIMAL_BINARY,
  95 |         "-in", str(input_alignment),
  96 |         "-out", str(output_alignment),
  97 |         "-automated1"
  98 |     ]
  99 |     
 100 |     log_file = output_alignment.parent.parent / "logs" / "alignment.log"
 101 |     
 102 |     if job_id:
 103 |         # Publish command line (displayed in green)
 104 |         from app.workers.events import publish_command
 105 |         publish_command(job_id, "trim", cmd)
 106 |         
 107 |         exit_code, stats = run_command_streaming(
 108 |             cmd,
 109 |             stderr_path=log_file,
 110 |             on_stdout_line=_make_log_callback(job_id, "trim", "stdout"),
 111 |             on_stderr_line=_make_log_callback(job_id, "trim", "stderr"),
 112 |         )
 113 |         
 114 |         if exit_code != 0:
 115 |             raise RuntimeError(f"trimAl failed with exit code {exit_code}")
 116 |     else:
 117 |         returncode, stdout, stderr = run_command(cmd, log_file=log_file)
 118 |         
 119 |         if returncode != 0:
 120 |             raise RuntimeError(f"trimAl failed with return code {returncode}. See logs.")
 121 | 
 122 | 
 123 | def _run_bmge(
 124 |     input_alignment: Path,
 125 |     output_alignment: Path,
 126 |     config: Config,
 127 |     logger,
 128 |     job_id: Optional[str] = None
 129 | ):
 130 |     """
 131 |     Run BMGE alignment trimming.
 132 |     
 133 |     Handles both JAR file and binary executable configurations.
 134 |     """
 135 |     bmge_bin = config.BMGE_BINARY
 136 |     cmd = []
 137 |     
 138 |     if str(bmge_bin).endswith(".jar"):
 139 |         cmd = ["java", "-jar", bmge_bin]
 140 |     else:
 141 |         cmd = [bmge_bin]
 142 |         
 143 |     cmd.extend([
 144 |         "-i", str(input_alignment),
 145 |         "-t", "DNA",
 146 |         "-of", str(output_alignment)
 147 |     ])
 148 |     
 149 |     log_file = output_alignment.parent.parent / "logs" / "alignment.log"
 150 |     
 151 |     if job_id:
 152 |         # Publish command line (displayed in green)
 153 |         from app.workers.events import publish_command
 154 |         publish_command(job_id, "trim", cmd)
 155 |         
 156 |         exit_code, stats = run_command_streaming(
 157 |             cmd,
 158 |             stderr_path=log_file,
 159 |             on_stdout_line=_make_log_callback(job_id, "trim", "stdout"),
 160 |             on_stderr_line=_make_log_callback(job_id, "trim", "stderr"),
 161 |         )
 162 |         
 163 |         if exit_code != 0:
 164 |             raise RuntimeError(f"BMGE failed with exit code {exit_code}")
 165 |     else:
 166 |         returncode, stdout, stderr = run_command(cmd, log_file=log_file)
 167 |         
 168 |         if returncode != 0:
 169 |             raise RuntimeError(f"BMGE failed with return code {returncode}. See logs.")

   1 | from flask import render_template
   2 | from flask_login import login_required, current_user
   3 | from app.user import bp
   4 | from app.models import Job
   5 | 
   6 | @bp.route('/jobs')
   7 | @login_required
   8 | def user_jobs():
   9 |     jobs = Job.query.filter_by(user_id=current_user.id).order_by(Job.created_at.desc()).all()
  10 |     return render_template('user_jobs.html', jobs=jobs)
  11 | 
  12 | @bp.route('/jobs/clear', methods=['POST'])
  13 | @login_required
  14 | def clear_jobs():
  15 |     import shutil
  16 |     import os
  17 |     from flask import flash, redirect, url_for
  18 |     from app.extensions import db
  19 |     
  20 |     jobs = Job.query.filter_by(user_id=current_user.id).all()
  21 |     count = 0
  22 |     for job in jobs:
  23 |         if job.job_dir and os.path.exists(job.job_dir):
  24 |             try:
  25 |                 if os.path.isdir(job.job_dir):
  26 |                     shutil.rmtree(job.job_dir)
  27 |                 else:
  28 |                     os.remove(job.job_dir)
  29 |             except Exception as e:
  30 |                 # Log error but continue
  31 |                 print(f"Error deleting job dir {job.job_dir}: {e}")
  32 |         
  33 |         db.session.delete(job)
  34 |         count += 1
  35 |         
  36 |     db.session.commit()
  37 |     flash(f'{count} jobs cleared successfully.', 'success')
  38 |     return redirect(url_for('user.user_jobs'))

FILE: app/workers/events.py

   1 | """
   2 | Real-time job event publishing module.
   3 | 
   4 | Provides utilities for publishing SSE events from the worker to Flask
   5 | via Redis PubSub. All events are JSON-encoded and include timestamps.
   6 | 
   7 | Event Types:
   8 | - job_state: Job lifecycle (running, completed, failed)
   9 | - step_start/step_done/step_failed: Pipeline step lifecycle
  10 | - log: Streaming log lines from tools
  11 | - overview: Human-friendly status messages
  12 | - metric: Optional metrics (bootstrap count, alignment stats, etc.)
  13 | 
  14 | Pipeline Steps (stable keys):
  15 | - input: Input Processing
  16 | - orient: Orientation Check (auto-fix reversed ITS sequences)
  17 | - blast: BLAST Search
  18 | - align: Alignment (MAFFT/MUSCLE/ClustalO)
  19 | - trim: Trimming (trimAl/BMGE)
  20 | - tree: Tree Building (NJ/RAxML/IQ-TREE/MrBayes)
  21 | - post: Post-Processing
  22 | """
  23 | 
  24 | import json
  25 | import logging
  26 | import time
  27 | import threading
  28 | from typing import Optional, List, Dict, Any
  29 | 
  30 | import redis
  31 | 
  32 | from app.config import Config
  33 | 
  34 | logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
  35 | 
  36 | # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  37 | # Constants
  38 | # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  39 | 
  40 | # Stable pipeline step keys
  41 | STEP_INPUT = "input"
  42 | STEP_ORIENT = "orient"
  43 | STEP_BLAST = "blast"
  44 | STEP_ALIGN = "align"
  45 | STEP_TRIM = "trim"
  46 | STEP_TREE = "tree"
  47 | STEP_POST = "post"
  48 | 
  49 | PIPELINE_STEPS = [STEP_INPUT, STEP_ORIENT, STEP_BLAST, STEP_ALIGN, STEP_TRIM, STEP_TREE, STEP_POST]
  50 | 
  51 | # Step states
  52 | STATE_QUEUED = "queued"
  53 | STATE_RUNNING = "running"
  54 | STATE_DONE = "done"
  55 | STATE_SKIPPED = "skipped"
  56 | STATE_FAILED = "failed"
  57 | 
  58 | # Event types
  59 | EVENT_JOB_STATE = "job_state"
  60 | EVENT_STEP_START = "step_start"
  61 | EVENT_STEP_DONE = "step_done"
  62 | EVENT_STEP_FAILED = "step_failed"
  63 | EVENT_LOG = "log"
  64 | EVENT_OVERVIEW = "overview"
  65 | EVENT_METRIC = "metric"
  66 | 
  67 | # Rate limiting settings
  68 | MAX_LOG_LINE_LENGTH = 4096  # 4KB max per line
  69 | MAX_LINES_PER_SECOND = 200  # Rate limit per (job_id, step, stream)
  70 | OVERFLOW_WARNING_INTERVAL = 5.0  # Seconds between overflow warnings
  71 | 
  72 | # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  73 | # Redis Connection (worker-side, no Flask app context)
  74 | # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  75 | 
  76 | _redis_client: Optional[redis.Redis] = None
  77 | _redis_lock = threading.Lock()
  78 | 
  79 | 
  80 | def _get_redis() -> redis.Redis:
  81 |     """Get or create Redis client for worker context (no Flask app)."""
  82 |     global _redis_client
  83 |     if _redis_client is None:
  84 |         with _redis_lock:
  85 |             if _redis_client is None:
  86 |                 _redis_client = redis.from_url(Config.REDIS_URL)
  87 |     return _redis_client
  88 | 
  89 | 
  90 | def get_event_channel(job_id: str) -> str:
  91 |     """Get Redis PubSub channel name for a job."""
  92 |     return f"job:{job_id}:events"
  93 | 
  94 | 
  95 | # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  96 | # Rate Limiting
  97 | # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  98 | 
  99 | class RateLimiter:
 100 |     """
 101 |     Simple token bucket rate limiter for log lines.
 102 |     
 103 |     Tracks tokens per (job_id, step, stream) tuple.
 104 |     Refills at MAX_LINES_PER_SECOND tokens per second.
 105 |     """
 106 |     
 107 |     def __init__(self):
 108 |         self._buckets: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]] = {}
 109 |         self._lock = threading.Lock()
 110 |         self._last_overflow_warning: Dict[str, float] = {}
 111 |     
 112 |     def _bucket_key(self, job_id: str, step: str, stream: str) -> str:
 113 |         return f"{job_id}:{step}:{stream}"
 114 |     
 115 |     def try_consume(self, job_id: str, step: str, stream: str) -> bool:
 116 |         """
 117 |         Try to consume one token. Returns True if allowed, False if rate limited.
 118 |         """
 119 |         key = self._bucket_key(job_id, step, stream)
 120 |         now = time.time()
 121 |         
 122 |         with self._lock:
 123 |             if key not in self._buckets:
 124 |                 self._buckets[key] = {
 125 |                     "tokens": MAX_LINES_PER_SECOND,
 126 |                     "last_refill": now
 127 |                 }
 128 |             
 129 |             bucket = self._buckets[key]
 130 |             
 131 |             # Refill tokens based on elapsed time
 132 |             elapsed = now - bucket["last_refill"]
 133 |             refill = elapsed * MAX_LINES_PER_SECOND
 134 |             bucket["tokens"] = min(MAX_LINES_PER_SECOND, bucket["tokens"] + refill)
 135 |             bucket["last_refill"] = now
 136 |             
 137 |             if bucket["tokens"] >= 1:
 138 |                 bucket["tokens"] -= 1
 139 |                 return True
 140 |             return False
 141 |     
 142 |     def should_warn_overflow(self, job_id: str, step: str, stream: str) -> bool:
 143 |         """Check if we should emit an overflow warning (throttled)."""
 144 |         key = self._bucket_key(job_id, step, stream)
 145 |         now = time.time()
 146 |         
 147 |         with self._lock:
 148 |             last_warning = self._last_overflow_warning.get(key, 0)
 149 |             if now - last_warning >= OVERFLOW_WARNING_INTERVAL:
 150 |                 self._last_overflow_warning[key] = now
 151 |                 return True
 152 |             return False
 153 | 
 154 | 
 155 | _rate_limiter = RateLimiter()
 156 | 
 157 | 
 158 | # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 159 | # Core Publishing Functions
 160 | # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 161 | 
 162 | def publish_event(job_id: str, payload: dict) -> None:
 163 |     """
 164 |     Publish an event to Redis PubSub channel.
 165 |     
 166 |     Automatically adds:
 167 |     - ts: Unix timestamp (float)
 168 |     - job_id: Job identifier
 169 |     
 170 |     Args:
 171 |         job_id: Job identifier
 172 |         payload: Event payload dict (must include 'type')
 173 |     """
 174 |     event = {
 175 |         **payload,
 176 |         "ts": time.time(),
 177 |         "job_id": job_id,
 178 |     }
 179 |     
 180 |     channel = get_event_channel(job_id)
 181 |     message = json.dumps(event)
 182 |     
 183 |     try:
 184 |         _get_redis().publish(channel, message)
 185 |     except Exception as e:
 186 |         # Log but don't fail the job
 187 |         logger.warning(f"Failed to publish event: {e}")
 188 | 
 189 | 
 190 | def publish_log(job_id: str, step: str, stream: str, line: str) -> None:
 191 |     """
 192 |     Publish a log line event with rate limiting and truncation.
 193 |     
 194 |     Args:
 195 |         job_id: Job identifier
 196 |         step: Pipeline step key (align, tree, etc.)
 197 |         stream: 'stdout' or 'stderr'
 198 |         line: Log line content
 199 |     """
 200 |     # Truncate long lines
 201 |     if len(line) > MAX_LOG_LINE_LENGTH:
 202 |         line = line[:MAX_LOG_LINE_LENGTH - 20] + "... (truncated)"
 203 |     
 204 |     # Strip trailing whitespace
 205 |     line = line.rstrip()
 206 |     
 207 |     # Skip empty lines
 208 |     if not line:
 209 |         return
 210 |     
 211 |     # Rate limiting
 212 |     if not _rate_limiter.try_consume(job_id, step, stream):
 213 |         # Rate limited - check if we should warn
 214 |         if _rate_limiter.should_warn_overflow(job_id, step, stream):
 215 |             # Get log file name for the step
 216 |             log_file = "pipeline.log"
 217 |             if step == STEP_ALIGN:
 218 |                 log_file = "alignment.log"
 219 |             elif step == STEP_TREE:
 220 |                 log_file = "tree_builder.log"
 221 |             
 222 |             publish_overview(
 223 |                 job_id,
 224 |                 f"(Log output truncated due to high volume; see {log_file} for full output)"
 225 |             )
 226 |         return
 227 |     
 228 |     publish_event(job_id, {
 229 |         "type": EVENT_LOG,
 230 |         "step": step,
 231 |         "stream": stream,
 232 |         "line": line,
 233 |     })
 234 | 
 235 | 
 236 | def publish_command(job_id: str, step: str, args: list) -> None:
 237 |     """
 238 |     Publish a command line event (displayed in green in the terminal).
 239 |     
 240 |     Args:
 241 |         job_id: Job identifier
 242 |         step: Pipeline step key (align, tree, etc.)
 243 |         args: Command arguments list (will be joined with spaces)
 244 |     """
 245 |     cmd_line = " ".join(str(arg) for arg in args)
 246 |     
 247 |     publish_event(job_id, {
 248 |         "type": EVENT_LOG,
 249 |         "step": step,
 250 |         "stream": "cmd",
 251 |         "line": f"$ {cmd_line}",
 252 |     })
 253 |

 255 | def publish_step_start(
 256 |     job_id: str,
 257 |     step: str,
 258 |     label: str,
 259 |     detail: str = "",
 260 |     tool: str = ""
 261 | ) -> None:
 262 |     """
 263 |     Publish step_start event.
 264 |     
 265 |     Args:
 266 |         job_id: Job identifier
 267 |         step: Pipeline step key
 268 |         label: Human-readable label (e.g., "Alignment (MAFFT)")
 269 |         detail: Additional detail (e.g., "8 threads, --auto")
 270 |         tool: Tool name (e.g., "mafft", "raxml-ng")
 271 |     """
 272 |     payload = {
 273 |         "type": EVENT_STEP_START,
 274 |         "step": step,
 275 |         "label": label,
 276 |     }
 277 |     if detail:
 278 |         payload["detail"] = detail
 279 |     if tool:
 280 |         payload["tool"] = tool
 281 |     
 282 |     publish_event(job_id, payload)
 283 |

 285 | def publish_step_done(job_id: str, step: str, detail: str = "") -> None:
 286 |     """
 287 |     Publish step_done event.
 288 |     
 289 |     Args:
 290 |         job_id: Job identifier
 291 |         step: Pipeline step key
 292 |         detail: Summary detail (e.g., "8 sequences, 450 columns")
 293 |     """
 294 |     payload = {
 295 |         "type": EVENT_STEP_DONE,
 296 |         "step": step,
 297 |     }
 298 |     if detail:
 299 |         payload["detail"] = detail
 300 |     
 301 |     publish_event(job_id, payload)

 304 | def publish_step_failed(
 305 |     job_id: str,
 306 |     step: str,
 307 |     error: str,
 308 |     tool: str = "",
 309 |     exit_code: Optional[int] = None,
 310 |     stderr_tail: Optional[List[str]] = None
 311 | ) -> None:
 312 |     """
 313 |     Publish step_failed event with rich context.
 314 |     
 315 |     Args:
 316 |         job_id: Job identifier
 317 |         step: Pipeline step key
 318 |         error: Error message
 319 |         tool: Tool name that failed
 320 |         exit_code: Process exit code if available
 321 |         stderr_tail: Last ~30 lines of stderr for debugging
 322 |     """
 323 |     payload = {
 324 |         "type": EVENT_STEP_FAILED,
 325 |         "step": step,
 326 |         "error": error,
 327 |     }
 328 |     if tool:
 329 |         payload["tool"] = tool
 330 |     if exit_code is not None:
 331 |         payload["exit_code"] = exit_code
 332 |     if stderr_tail:
 333 |         payload["stderr_tail"] = stderr_tail[:30]  # Cap at 30 lines
 334 |     
 335 |     publish_event(job_id, payload)
 336 |

 404 | def publish_job_failed(
 405 |     job_id: str,
 406 |     failed_step: str,
 407 |     failed_step_label: str,
 408 |     error_summary: str,
 409 |     tool: str = "",
 410 |     exit_code: Optional[int] = None,
 411 |     stderr_tail: Optional[List[str]] = None
 412 | ) -> None:
 413 |     """
 414 |     Publish job_state:failed event with full context.
 415 |     
 416 |     Args:
 417 |         job_id: Job identifier
 418 |         failed_step: Step key that failed

 442 | # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 443 | # Job Meta Helpers
 444 | # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 445 | 
 446 | def get_initial_steps_meta() -> Dict[str, dict]:
 447 |     """
 448 |     Get initial steps metadata structure for job.meta['steps'].
 449 |     
 450 |     Returns:
 451 |         Dict with all pipeline steps in 'queued' state
 452 |     """
 453 |     return {
 454 |         STEP_INPUT: {"label": "Input Processing", "state": STATE_QUEUED},
 455 |         STEP_ORIENT: {"label": "Orientation Check", "state": STATE_QUEUED},
 456 |         STEP_BLAST: {"label": "BLAST Search", "state": STATE_QUEUED},
 457 |         STEP_ALIGN: {"label": "Alignment", "state": STATE_QUEUED},
 458 |         STEP_TRIM: {"label": "Trimming", "state": STATE_QUEUED},
 459 |         STEP_TREE: {"label": "Tree Building", "state": STATE_QUEUED},
 460 |         STEP_POST: {"label": "Post-Processing", "state": STATE_QUEUED},
 461 |     }
 462 | 
 463 | 
 464 | def update_job_meta(job, patch: dict) -> None:
 465 |     """
 466 |     Update RQ job.meta with patch dict and save.
 467 |     
 468 |     Args:
 469 |         job: RQ job object (from get_current_job())
 470 |         patch: Dict of keys to update in job.meta
 471 |     """
 472 |     if job is None:
 473 |         return
 474 |     
 475 |     for key, value in patch.items():
 476 |         job.meta[key] = value
 477 |     job.save_meta()
 478 | 
 479 | 
 480 | def update_step_meta(job, step: str, patch: dict) -> None:
 481 |     """
 482 |     Update a specific step in job.meta['steps'] and save.
 483 |     
 484 |     Args:
 485 |         job: RQ job object
 486 |         step: Pipeline step key
 487 |         patch: Dict of keys to update in the step's metadata
 488 |     """
 489 |     if job is None:
 490 |         return
 491 |     
 492 |     if "steps" not in job.meta:
 493 |         job.meta["steps"] = get_initial_steps_meta()
 494 |     
 495 |     if step in job.meta["steps"]:
 496 |         job.meta["steps"][step].update(patch)
 497 |     else:
 498 |         job.meta["steps"][step] = patch
 499 |     
 500 |     job.save_meta()

   1 | import redis
   2 | from rq import Queue
   3 | from flask import current_app
   4 | from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
   5 | 
   6 | def get_redis_connection():
   7 |     redis_url = current_app.config.get('REDIS_URL', 'redis://localhost:6379/0')
   8 |     return redis.from_url(redis_url)
   9 | 
  10 | def get_queue(name='phylo_jobs') -> Queue:
  11 |     conn = get_redis_connection()
  12 |     return Queue(name, connection=conn)
  13 | 
  14 | def enqueue_job(job_params: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
  15 |     """Enqueue a phylo analysis job and return the job ID."""
  16 |     q = get_queue()
  17 |     from app.workers.tasks import run_phylo_job
  18 |     job = q.enqueue(run_phylo_job, job_params, job_timeout='1h')
  19 |     return job.id
  20 | 
  21 | def get_job_status(job_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  22 |     """Return a dict with at least: id, status, error (optional), progress (optional)."""
  23 |     try:
  24 |         q = get_queue()
  25 |         job = q.fetch_job(job_id)
  26 |         
  27 |         if job is None:
  28 |             return {"id": job_id, "status": "unknown", "error": "Job not found"}
  29 |         
  30 |         status = job.get_status()
  31 |         result = job.result
  32 |         
  33 |         response = {
  34 |             "id": job_id,
  35 |             "status": status,
  36 |             "enqueued_at": job.enqueued_at.isoformat() if job.enqueued_at else None,
  37 |             "started_at": job.started_at.isoformat() if job.started_at else None,
  38 |             "ended_at": job.ended_at.isoformat() if job.ended_at else None,
  39 |         }
  40 |         
  41 |         if job.exc_info:
  42 |             response["error"] = job.exc_info
  43 |             
  44 |         if result:
  45 |             response["result"] = result
  46 |             
  47 |         return response
  48 |         
  49 |     except Exception as e:
  50 |         return {"id": job_id, "status": "error", "error": str(e)}

   1 | """
   2 | Phylo job worker task.
   3 | 
   4 | This module contains the main RQ task for running phylogenetic analysis jobs.
   5 | It publishes real-time events via Redis PubSub for the SSE status dashboard.
   6 | """
   7 | 
   8 | import copy
   9 | import json
  10 | import logging
  11 | import time
  12 | import traceback
  13 | from datetime import datetime, timezone
  14 | 
  15 | from rq import get_current_job
  16 | 
  17 | from app.config import Config
  18 | from app.workers.events import (
  19 |     STEP_INPUT, STEP_ORIENT, STEP_BLAST, STEP_ALIGN, STEP_TRIM, STEP_TREE, STEP_POST,
  20 |     STATE_QUEUED, STATE_RUNNING, STATE_DONE, STATE_SKIPPED, STATE_FAILED,
  21 |     get_initial_steps_meta,
  22 |     publish_job_running, publish_job_completed, publish_job_failed,
  23 |     publish_step_start, publish_step_done, publish_step_failed,
  24 |     publish_overview, publish_log, publish_metric,
  25 |     update_job_meta, update_step_meta,
  26 | )
  27 | 
  28 | # Configure logging for the worker
  29 | logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
  30 | logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
  31 | 
  32 | 
  33 | def parse_fasta_records(fasta_text: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
  34 |     """
  35 |     Returns list of (header_without_gt, sequence_string_no_whitespace).
  36 |     Assumes FASTA headers start with '>'.
  37 |     """
  38 |     records: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
  39 |     header: str | None = None
  40 |     seq_chunks: list[str] = []
  41 | 
  42 |     for raw_line in fasta_text.splitlines():
  43 |         line = raw_line.strip()
  44 |         if not line:
  45 |             continue
  46 | 
  47 |         if line.startswith(">"):
  48 |             # flush previous record
  49 |             if header is not None:
  50 |                 seq = "".join(seq_chunks)
  51 |                 seq = "".join(seq.split())  # remove ALL whitespace
  52 |                 records.append((header, seq))
  53 | 
  54 |             header = line[1:].strip()
  55 |             seq_chunks = []
  56 |         else:
  57 |             seq_chunks.append(line)
  58 | 
  59 |     # flush final record
  60 |     if header is not None:
  61 |         seq = "".join(seq_chunks)
  62 |         seq = "".join(seq.split())
  63 |         records.append((header, seq))
  64 | 
  65 |     return records
  66 | 
  67 | 
  68 | def dedupe_and_uniquify_fasta(fasta_text: str) -> tuple[str, dict]:
  69 |     """
  70 |     - Drops records that are exact duplicates of a prior record (same full header AND same sequence).
  71 |     - Ensures unique record IDs (first token of header). If repeated, appends _2, _3...
  72 |     Returns: (new_fasta_text, stats dict)
  73 |     """
  74 |     records = parse_fasta_records(fasta_text)
  75 | 
  76 |     seen_exact: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
  77 |     id_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
  78 | 
  79 |     kept: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
  80 |     dropped_exact = 0
  81 |     renamed = 0
  82 | 
  83 |     for header, seq in records:
  84 |         key = (header, seq)
  85 |         if key in seen_exact:
  86 |             dropped_exact += 1
  87 |             continue
  88 |         seen_exact.add(key)
  89 | 
  90 |         # split header into id + description
  91 |         if header.strip():
  92 |             parts = header.split(None, 1)
  93 |             rec_id = parts[0]
  94 |             desc = parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else ""
  95 |         else:
  96 |             rec_id = "seq"
  97 |             desc = ""
  98 |         
  99 |         # Cap header lengths to prevent abuse (e.g., 200KB pasted headers)
 100 |         MAX_SEQ_ID_LEN = 100
 101 |         MAX_DESC_LEN = 300
 102 |         rec_id = rec_id[:MAX_SEQ_ID_LEN]
 103 |         desc = desc[:MAX_DESC_LEN]
 104 | 
 105 |         # make ID unique
 106 |         id_counts[rec_id] = id_counts.get(rec_id, 0) + 1
 107 |         n = id_counts[rec_id]
 108 |         if n > 1:
 109 |             new_id = f"{rec_id}_{n}"
 110 |             renamed += 1
 111 |         else:
 112 |             new_id = rec_id
 113 | 
 114 |         new_header = f"{new_id} {desc}".rstrip()
 115 |         kept.append((new_header, seq))
 116 | 
 117 |     # write back out with wrapped sequence lines (optional; here: 80 cols)
 118 |     out_lines: list[str] = []
 119 |     for header, seq in kept:
 120 |         out_lines.append(f">{header}")
 121 |         for i in range(0, len(seq), 80):
 122 |             out_lines.append(seq[i:i+80])
 123 | 
 124 |     stats = {
 125 |         "input_records": len(records),
 126 |         "kept_records": len(kept),
 127 |         "dropped_exact_duplicates": dropped_exact,
 128 |         "renamed_due_to_duplicate_ids": renamed,
 129 |     }
 130 |     return "\n".join(out_lines) + "\n", stats
 131 | 
 132 | 
 133 | def fasta_record_count(fasta_text: str) -> int:
 134 |     """Count FASTA headers. Sufficient for deciding BLAST policy."""
 135 |     return sum(1 for line in fasta_text.splitlines() if line.startswith(">"))
 136 | 
 137 | 
 138 | def _normalize_input_type(input_type: str | None) -> str | None:
 139 |     aliases = {
 140 |         "beginner": "pasted_sequence",   # Beginner Quick Tree sends pasted FASTA in job_params["sequence"]
 141 |         "sequence": "pasted_sequence",
 142 |         "accessions": "accession_list",
 143 |         "fasta": "fasta_upload",
 144 |     }
 145 |     return aliases.get(input_type, input_type)
 146 | 
 147 | 
 148 | def _normalize_blast_mode(blast_mode: str | None) -> str:
 149 |     """
 150 |     blast_mode:
 151 |       - "auto" (default): BLAST only if exactly one query sequence/accession
 152 |       - "on": force BLAST (still refused for multi-query inputs per your policy)
 153 |       - "off": never BLAST
 154 |     """
 155 |     mode = (blast_mode or "auto").strip().lower()
 156 |     if mode not in ("auto", "on", "off"):
 157 |         return "auto"
 158 |     return mode
 159 | 
 160 | 
 161 | def _should_blast_single_only(blast_mode: str, n_queries: int) -> bool:
 162 |     """
 163 |     Your policy:
 164 |       - Never BLAST multiple queries (multi FASTA or multiple accessions)
 165 |       - BLAST only when there's exactly 1 query, unless blast_mode="off"
 166 |       - blast_mode="on" still must respect the "single only" rule (we enforce below)
 167 |     """
 168 |     if blast_mode == "off":
 169 |         return False
 170 |     return n_queries == 1  # for "auto" and effectively for "on" too
 171 | 
 172 | 
 173 | def _count_alignment_stats(fasta_path) -> tuple[int, int]:
 174 |     """Count sequences and alignment columns from a FASTA file."""
 175 |     try:
 176 |         with open(fasta_path, 'r') as f:
 177 |             content = f.read()
 178 |         records = parse_fasta_records(content)
 179 |         if not records:
 180 |             return 0, 0
 181 |         n_seqs = len(records)
 182 |         n_cols = len(records[0][1]) if records else 0
 183 |         return n_seqs, n_cols
 184 |     except Exception:
 185 |         return 0, 0
 186 |

 188 | def run_phylo_job(job_params: dict) -> dict:
 189 |     """
 190 |     Main phylogenetic analysis job.
 191 |     
 192 |     Publishes real-time events for the SSE status dashboard.
 193 |     Uses centralized exception handling - no early returns on error.
 194 |     """
 195 |     job = get_current_job()
 196 |     job_id = job.id if job else "local_debug"
 197 |     
 198 |     # Track current step for error reporting
 199 |     current_step = None
 200 |     current_tool = None
 201 |     current_step_label = None
 202 |     
 203 |     # Stats from streaming command (for error context)
 204 |     last_stats = {}
 205 | 
 206 |     logger.info(f"Starting job {job_id} with params: {job_params}")
 207 | 
 208 |     # 1. Create Job Directory
 209 |     job_dir = Config.JOB_DIR / job_id
 210 |     job_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 211 | 
 212 |     # Create subdirectories
 213 |     (job_dir / "input").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 214 |     (job_dir / "blast").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 215 |     (job_dir / "alignment").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 216 |     (job_dir / "tree").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 217 |     (job_dir / "logs").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 218 | 
 219 |     # 2. Save job_params
 220 |     input_info_path = job_dir / "input_info.json"
 221 |     with open(input_info_path, "w") as f:
 222 |         json.dump(job_params, f, indent=2)
 223 | 
 224 |     # 2b. Initialize with single app context
 225 |     from app import create_app
 226 |     from app.extensions import db
 227 |     from app.models import Job
 228 | 
 229 |     _app = create_app()
 230 |     
 231 |     # Per-job pipeline log
 232 |     log_file = job_dir / "logs" / "pipeline.log"
 233 |     file_handler = logging.FileHandler(log_file)
 234 |     file_handler.setFormatter(logging.Formatter("%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"))
 235 |     logger.addHandler(file_handler)
 236 | 
 237 |     input_raw_path = job_dir / "input" / "input_raw.fasta"
 238 |     blast_result = None
 239 | 
 240 |     try:
 241 |         # Use single app context for all DB operations
 242 |         with _app.app_context():
 243 |             # Initialize DB status
 244 |             db_job = Job.query.get(job_id)
 245 |             if db_job:
 246 |                 metrics = db_job.metrics or {}
 247 |                 metrics["started_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
 248 |                 
 249 |                 # Check for validation warnings passed from API
 250 |                 validation_warnings = job_params.get("validation_warnings", [])
 251 |                 if validation_warnings:
 252 |                     metrics["validation_warnings"] = validation_warnings
 253 |                     logger.warning(f"Job started with params warnings: {validation_warnings}")
 254 | 
 255 |                 db_job.metrics = metrics
 256 |                 db_job.status = "running"
 257 |                 db.session.commit()
 258 |             
 259 |             # Initialize job meta for SSE
 260 |             if job:
 261 |                 job.meta["steps"] = get_initial_steps_meta()
 262 |                 job.meta["current_step"] = None
 263 |                 job.meta["current_tool"] = None
 264 |                 job.meta["started_at"] = time.time()
 265 |                 
 266 |                 # Pre-mark optional steps as skipped based on job parameters
 267 |                 # This ensures the UI shows correct pipeline from the start
 268 |                 input_type = _normalize_input_type(job_params.get("input_type"))
 269 |                 blast_mode = _normalize_blast_mode(job_params.get("blast_mode"))
 270 |                 trim_method = job_params.get("trimming_method", "none")
 271 |                 if trim_method == "default":
 272 |                     trim_method = Config.BEGINNER_DEFAULT_TRIMMING
 273 |                 
 274 |                 # BLAST is only used for single accession with optional blast
 275 |                 will_do_blast = (input_type == "accession" and blast_mode == "optional")
 276 |                 if not will_do_blast:
 277 |                     job.meta["steps"][STEP_BLAST]["state"] = STATE_SKIPPED
 278 |                     job.meta["steps"][STEP_BLAST]["label"] = "BLAST Search (skipped)"
 279 |                 
 280 |                 # Trim is skipped if method is none
 281 |                 if not trim_method or trim_method.lower() == "none":
 282 |                     job.meta["steps"][STEP_TRIM]["state"] = STATE_SKIPPED
 283 |                     job.meta["steps"][STEP_TRIM]["label"] = "Trimming (skipped)"
 284 |                 
 285 |                 job.save_meta()
 286 |             
 287 |             # Publish job running
 288 |             publish_job_running(job_id)
 289 |             publish_overview(job_id, "Starting pipeline...")
 290 |             
 291 |             # =========================================================
 292 |             # STEP: INPUT PROCESSING
 293 |             # =========================================================
 294 |             current_step = STEP_INPUT
 295 |             current_step_label = "Input Processing"
 296 |             current_tool = None
 297 |             
 298 |             if job:
 299 |                 job.meta["current_step"] = current_step
 300 |                 job.save_meta()
 301 |             
 302 |             publish_step_start(job_id, STEP_INPUT, "Input Processing", "Validating input data")
 303 |             update_step_meta(job, STEP_INPUT, {"state": STATE_RUNNING})
 304 |             
 305 |             input_type = _normalize_input_type(job_params.get("input_type"))
 306 |             blast_mode = _normalize_blast_mode(job_params.get("blast_mode"))
 307 |             do_blast = False
 308 |             n_records = 0
 309 | 
 310 |             if input_type == "accession_list":
 311 |                 accessions = job_params.get("accessions", []) or []
 312 |                 logger.info(f"Processing accession list: {accessions}")
 313 | 
 314 |                 if len(accessions) != 1:
 315 |                     raise ValueError("accession_list jobs must contain exactly one accession (refusing to BLAST multiple).")
 316 | 
 317 |                 if blast_mode == "off":
 318 |                     raise ValueError("BLAST is disabled (blast_mode=off), but accession_list requires BLAST to fetch sequences.")
 319 | 
 320 |                 do_blast = True
 321 |                 n_records = 1
 322 | 
 323 |             elif input_type == "pasted_sequence":
 324 |                 sequence = (job_params.get("sequence") or "").strip()
 325 |                 logger.info("Processing pasted sequence")
 326 | 
 327 |                 if not sequence:
 328 |                     raise ValueError("No sequence provided for pasted_sequence input type")
 329 |                 if not sequence.lstrip().startswith(">"):
 330 |                     raise ValueError("Pasted sequence must be FASTA (must start with '>')")
 331 | 
 332 |                 # Always write what the user gave us
 333 |                 input_raw_path.write_text(sequence + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
 334 |                 logger.info(f"Wrote input FASTA: {input_raw_path} ({input_raw_path.stat().st_size} bytes)")
 335 | 
 336 |                 n_records = fasta_record_count(sequence)
 337 |                 logger.info(f"Input FASTA records: {n_records}")
 338 | 
 339 |                 # Optional BLAST only if exactly 1 record
 340 |                 if blast_mode == "on" and n_records != 1:
 341 |                     raise ValueError("Refusing to BLAST multi-sequence FASTA (blast_mode=on requested). Provide exactly 1 record.")
 342 | 
 343 |                 do_blast = _should_blast_single_only(blast_mode, n_records)
 344 | 
 345 |             elif input_type == "fasta_upload":
 346 |                 logger.info("Processing FASTA upload")
 347 | 
 348 |                 if not input_raw_path.exists():
 349 |                     raise FileNotFoundError(f"Expected uploaded FASTA at {input_raw_path} but it does not exist")
 350 | 
 351 |                 uploaded = input_raw_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
 352 |                 n_records = fasta_record_count(uploaded)
 353 |                 logger.info(f"Uploaded FASTA records: {n_records}")
 354 | 
 355 |                 if blast_mode == "on" and n_records != 1:
 356 |                     raise ValueError("Refusing to BLAST multi-sequence FASTA upload (blast_mode=on requested). Provide exactly 1 record.")
 357 | 
 358 |                 do_blast = _should_blast_single_only(blast_mode, n_records)
 359 | 
 360 |             else:
 361 |                 raise ValueError(f"Unknown input type: {input_type}")
 362 | 
 363 |             publish_step_done(job_id, STEP_INPUT, f"{n_records} sequence(s) validated")
 364 |             publish_overview(job_id, f"Input validated: {n_records} sequence(s)")
 365 |             update_step_meta(job, STEP_INPUT, {"state": STATE_DONE, "detail": f"{n_records} sequences"})
 366 | 
 367 |             # =========================================================
 368 |             # STEP: BLAST (optional)
 369 |             # =========================================================
 370 |             current_step = STEP_BLAST
 371 |             current_step_label = "BLAST Search"
 372 |             
 373 |             if job:
 374 |                 job.meta["current_step"] = current_step
 375 |                 job.save_meta()
 376 | 
 377 |             if do_blast:
 378 |                 publish_step_start(job_id, STEP_BLAST, "BLAST Search", "Searching NCBI database")
 379 |                 update_step_meta(job, STEP_BLAST, {"state": STATE_RUNNING})
 380 |                 
 381 |                 if input_type == "accession_list":
 382 |                     accessions = job_params.get("accessions", [])
 383 |                     from app.services.blast_service import blast_from_accessions
 384 |                     blast_result = blast_from_accessions(accessions, Config, logger)
 385 |                 else:
 386 |                     sequence = input_raw_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
 387 |                     from app.services.blast_service import blast_from_sequence
 388 |                     blast_result = blast_from_sequence(sequence, Config, logger)
 389 |                 
 390 |                 if blast_result:
 391 |                     with open(job_dir / "blast" / "blast_results.json", "w") as f:
 392 |                         json.dump(blast_result, f, indent=2)
 393 | 
 394 |                     fasta_path = blast_result.get("fasta_path")
 395 |                     if fasta_path:
 396 |                         with open(fasta_path, "r") as src, open(job_dir / "blast" / "blast_sequences.fasta", "w") as dst:
 397 |                             dst.write(src.read())
 398 |                         with open(fasta_path, "r") as src:
 399 |                             input_raw_path.write_text(src.read(), encoding="utf-8")
 400 |                         logger.info(f"Replaced input FASTA with BLAST-expanded dataset from {fasta_path}")
 401 |                     
 402 |                     hit_count = blast_result.get("sequence_count", 0)
 403 |                     publish_step_done(job_id, STEP_BLAST, f"{hit_count} sequences found")
 404 |                     publish_overview(job_id, f"BLAST complete: {hit_count} related sequences found")
 405 |                     update_step_meta(job, STEP_BLAST, {"state": STATE_DONE, "detail": f"{hit_count} sequences"})
 406 |             else:
 407 |                 # BLAST skipped
 408 |                 skip_reason = "multi-sequence input" if n_records > 1 else "disabled"
 409 |                 update_step_meta(job, STEP_BLAST, {"state": STATE_SKIPPED, "label": "BLAST Search (skipped)"})
 410 |                 publish_overview(job_id, f"BLAST skipped ({skip_reason})")
 411 |                 logger.info(f"Skipping BLAST ({skip_reason})")
 412 | 
 413 |             # Final guard: alignment requires input_raw.fasta
 414 |             if not input_raw_path.exists() or input_raw_path.stat().st_size == 0:
 415 |                 raise FileNotFoundError(f"Missing or empty input FASTA: {input_raw_path}")
 416 | 
 417 |             # After input is finalized: ensure enough sequences to build alignment/tree
 418 |             final_fasta = input_raw_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
 419 |             final_fasta, stats = dedupe_and_uniquify_fasta(final_fasta)
 420 |             input_raw_path.write_text(final_fasta, encoding="utf-8")
 421 | 
 422 |             logger.info(
 423 |                 "FASTA cleanup: input_records=%s kept=%s dropped_exact=%s renamed_ids=%s",
 424 |                 stats["input_records"],
 425 |                 stats["kept_records"],
 426 |                 stats["dropped_exact_duplicates"],
 427 |                 stats["renamed_due_to_duplicate_ids"],
 428 |             )
 429 | 
 430 |             final_n = fasta_record_count(final_fasta)
 431 |             logger.info(f"Final FASTA records going into alignment: {final_n}")
 432 |             if final_n < 2:
 433 |                 raise ValueError("Need at least 2 sequences to build an alignment/tree")
 434 | 
 435 |             # =========================================================
 436 |             # STEP: ORIENTATION CHECK
 437 |             # =========================================================
 438 |             current_step = STEP_ORIENT
 439 |             current_step_label = "Orientation Check"
 440 |             current_tool = None
 441 |             
 442 |             if job:
 443 |                 job.meta["current_step"] = current_step
 444 |                 job.save_meta()
 445 |             
 446 |             publish_step_start(job_id, STEP_ORIENT, "Orientation Check", "Checking sequence orientations")
 447 |             update_step_meta(job, STEP_ORIENT, {"state": STATE_RUNNING})
 448 |             
 449 |             from app.services.orientation_service import fix_sequence_orientation
 450 |             
 451 |             orient_fasta = input_raw_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
 452 |             fixed_fasta, orient_stats = fix_sequence_orientation(orient_fasta)
 453 |             input_raw_path.write_text(fixed_fasta, encoding="utf-8")
 454 |             
 455 |             reversed_count = orient_stats.get("reverse", 0)
 456 |             uncertain_count = orient_stats.get("uncertain", 0)
 457 |             
 458 |             if reversed_count > 0:
 459 |                 orient_detail = f"{reversed_count} sequence(s) reverse complemented"
 460 |                 if uncertain_count > 0:
 461 |                     orient_detail += f", {uncertain_count} uncertain"
 462 |             elif uncertain_count > 0:
 463 |                 orient_detail = f"All forward, {uncertain_count} uncertain orientation"
 464 |             else:
 465 |                 orient_detail = "All sequences correctly oriented"
 466 |             
 467 |             logger.info(
 468 |                 "Orientation check: total=%s forward=%s reverse=%s uncertain=%s",
 469 |                 orient_stats.get("total", 0),
 470 |                 orient_stats.get("forward", 0),
 471 |                 reversed_count,
 472 |                 uncertain_count,
 473 |             )
 474 |             
 475 |             publish_step_done(job_id, STEP_ORIENT, orient_detail)
 476 |             update_step_meta(job, STEP_ORIENT, {"state": STATE_DONE, "detail": orient_detail})
 477 |             
 478 |             if reversed_count > 0:
 479 |                 publish_metric(job_id, STEP_ORIENT, "reversed", reversed_count)
 480 | 
 481 |             # =========================================================
 482 |             # STEP: ALIGNMENT
 483 |             # =========================================================
 484 |             current_step = STEP_ALIGN
 485 |             align_method = job_params.get("alignment_method", "default")
 486 |             if align_method == "default":
 487 |                 align_method = Config.BEGINNER_DEFAULT_ALIGNER
 488 |             
 489 |             current_tool = align_method.lower()
 490 |             current_step_label = f"Alignment ({align_method.upper()})"
 491 |             
 492 |             if job:
 493 |                 job.meta["current_step"] = current_step
 494 |                 job.meta["current_tool"] = current_tool
 495 |                 job.save_meta()
 496 |             
 497 |             alignment_raw_path = job_dir / "alignment" / "alignment_raw.fasta"
 498 |             alignment_trimmed_path = job_dir / "alignment" / "alignment_trimmed.fasta"
 499 |             
 500 |             threads = min(8, __import__('os').cpu_count() or 1)
 501 |             publish_step_start(job_id, STEP_ALIGN, current_step_label, f"{threads} threads", tool=current_tool)
 502 |             update_step_meta(job, STEP_ALIGN, {
 503 |                 "state": STATE_RUNNING,
 504 |                 "label": current_step_label,
 505 |                 "tool": current_tool
 506 |             })
 507 | 
 508 |             align_opts = job_params.get("alignment_options", {})
 509 |             # Pass tree_method so MAFFT can use faster settings for NJ
 510 |             align_opts["tree_method"] = job_params.get("tree_method", "nj")
 511 |             from app.models import AlignmentParams
 512 |             from app.services.alignment_service import run_alignment
 513 | 
 514 |             align_params = AlignmentParams(method=align_method, advanced_options=align_opts)
 515 |             run_alignment(input_raw_path, alignment_raw_path, align_params, Config, logger, job_id=job_id)
 516 | 
 517 |             n_seqs, n_cols = _count_alignment_stats(alignment_raw_path)
 518 |             detail = f"{n_seqs} sequences, {n_cols} columns"
 519 |             
 520 |             publish_step_done(job_id, STEP_ALIGN, detail)
 521 |             publish_overview(job_id, f"Alignment complete: {detail}")
 522 |             update_step_meta(job, STEP_ALIGN, {"state": STATE_DONE, "detail": detail})
 523 |             publish_metric(job_id, STEP_ALIGN, "sequences", n_seqs)
 524 |             publish_metric(job_id, STEP_ALIGN, "columns", n_cols)
 525 | 
 526 |             # =========================================================
 527 |             # STEP: TRIMMING
 528 |             # =========================================================
 529 |             current_step = STEP_TRIM
 530 |             trim_method = job_params.get("trimming_method", "none")
 531 |             if trim_method == "default":
 532 |                 trim_method = Config.BEGINNER_DEFAULT_TRIMMING
 533 |             
 534 |             if job:
 535 |                 job.meta["current_step"] = current_step
 536 |                 job.meta["current_tool"] = trim_method if trim_method != "none" else None
 537 |                 job.save_meta()
 538 |             
 539 |             if trim_method and trim_method.lower() != "none":
 540 |                 current_tool = trim_method.lower()
 541 |                 current_step_label = f"Trimming ({trim_method})"
 542 |                 
 543 |                 publish_step_start(job_id, STEP_TRIM, current_step_label, "", tool=current_tool)
 544 |                 update_step_meta(job, STEP_TRIM, {
 545 |                     "state": STATE_RUNNING,
 546 |                     "label": current_step_label,
 547 |                     "tool": current_tool
 548 |                 })
 549 | 
 550 |                 from app.services.trimming_service import run_trimming
 551 |                 run_trimming(alignment_raw_path, alignment_trimmed_path, trim_method, Config, logger, job_id=job_id)
 552 | 
 553 |                 _, trimmed_cols = _count_alignment_stats(alignment_trimmed_path)
 554 |                 detail = f"{trimmed_cols} columns retained"
 555 |                 
 556 |                 publish_step_done(job_id, STEP_TRIM, detail)
 557 |                 publish_overview(job_id, f"Trimming complete: {detail}")
 558 |                 update_step_meta(job, STEP_TRIM, {"state": STATE_DONE, "detail": detail})
 559 |             else:
 560 |                 # Trimming skipped
 561 |                 import shutil
 562 |                 shutil.copy(alignment_raw_path, alignment_trimmed_path)
 563 |                 update_step_meta(job, STEP_TRIM, {"state": STATE_SKIPPED, "label": "Trimming (skipped)"})
 564 |                 publish_overview(job_id, "Trimming skipped (method: none)")
 565 |                 current_tool = None
 566 |                 current_step_label = "Trimming (skipped)"
 567 | 
 568 |             # =========================================================
 569 |             # STEP: TREE BUILDING
 570 |             # =========================================================
 571 |             current_step = STEP_TREE
 572 |             tree_method = job_params.get("tree_method", "nj")
 573 |             tree_model = job_params.get("tree_model", Config.DEFAULT_ML_MODEL)
 574 |             bootstrap = int(job_params.get("bootstrap", Config.DEFAULT_BOOTSTRAPS))
 575 |             mcmc_gens = int(job_params.get("mcmc_generations", Config.DEFAULT_MCMC_GENERATIONS))
 576 |             
 577 |             current_tool = tree_method.lower()
 578 |             current_step_label = f"Tree Building ({tree_method.upper()})"
 579 |             
 580 |             if job:
 581 |                 job.meta["current_step"] = current_step
 582 |                 job.meta["current_tool"] = current_tool
 583 |                 job.save_meta()
 584 |             
 585 |             tree_newick_path = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_original.newick"
 586 |             tree_nexus_path = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_original.nexus"
 587 |             tree_metadata_path = job_dir / "tree" / "tree_metadata.json"
 588 |             
 589 |             detail_parts = [tree_model]
 590 |             if tree_method in ("raxml", "iqtree") and bootstrap:
 591 |                 detail_parts.append(f"{bootstrap} bootstraps")
 592 |             if tree_method == "mrbayes":
 593 |                 detail_parts.append(f"{mcmc_gens} generations")
 594 |             
 595 |             publish_step_start(job_id, STEP_TREE, current_step_label, ", ".join(detail_parts), tool=current_tool)
 596 |             update_step_meta(job, STEP_TREE, {
 597 |                 "state": STATE_RUNNING,
 598 |                 "label": current_step_label,
 599 |                 "tool": current_tool
 600 |             })
 601 | 
 602 |             from app.models import TreeBuilderParams
 603 |             from app.services.tree_builder_service import run_tree_builder
 604 | 
 605 |             tree_params = TreeBuilderParams(
 606 |                 method=tree_method,
 607 |                 model=tree_model,
 608 |                 bootstrap=bootstrap,
 609 |                 mcmc_generations=mcmc_gens,
 610 |                 # RAxML Params
 611 |                 run_preset=job_params.get("run_preset", "fast_good"),
 612 |                 bootstrap_preset=job_params.get("bootstrap_preset", "standard"),
 613 |                 bootstrap_cap=job_params.get("bootstrap_cap"),
 614 |                 enable_bootstrap=job_params.get("enable_bootstrap", True),
 615 |                 start_tree_override=job_params.get("start_tree_override"),
 616 |                 moose_enabled=job_params.get("moose_enabled", False),
 617 |                 early_stopping=job_params.get("early_stopping", False),
 618 |                 seed=job_params.get("seed"),
 619 |                 outgroup=job_params.get("outgroup")
 620 |             )
 621 | 
 622 |             metadata = run_tree_builder(
 623 |                 alignment_trimmed_path,
 624 |                 tree_newick_path,
 625 |                 tree_nexus_path,
 626 |                 tree_params,
 627 |                 Config,
 628 |                 logger,
 629 |                 job_id=job_id,
 630 |             )
 631 | 
 632 |             with open(tree_metadata_path, "w") as f:
 633 |                 json.dump(metadata, f, indent=2)
 634 | 
 635 |             from app.services.tree_edit_service import load_tree_state
 636 |             load_tree_state(job_dir)
 637 | 
 638 |             publish_step_done(job_id, STEP_TREE, "Tree generated")
 639 |             publish_overview(job_id, f"Tree built using {tree_method.upper()}")
 640 |             update_step_meta(job, STEP_TREE, {"state": STATE_DONE, "detail": "Tree generated"})
 641 | 
 642 |             # =========================================================
 643 |             # STEP: POST-PROCESSING
 644 |             # =========================================================
 645 |             current_step = STEP_POST
 646 |             current_step_label = "Post-Processing"
 647 |             current_tool = None
 648 |             
 649 |             if job:
 650 |                 job.meta["current_step"] = current_step
 651 |                 job.meta["current_tool"] = None
 652 |                 job.save_meta()
 653 |             
 654 |             publish_step_start(job_id, STEP_POST, "Post-Processing", "Generating output files")
 655 |             update_step_meta(job, STEP_POST, {"state": STATE_RUNNING})
 656 | 
 657 |             # All files are already generated, just finalize
 658 |             publish_step_done(job_id, STEP_POST, "All files ready")
 659 |             update_step_meta(job, STEP_POST, {"state": STATE_DONE})
 660 | 
 661 |             # =========================================================
 662 |             # JOB COMPLETED
 663 |             # =========================================================
 664 |             logger.info(f"Job {job_id} completed successfully.")
 665 |             
 666 |             # Update DB
 667 |             db_job = Job.query.get(job_id)
 668 |             if db_job:
 669 |                 metrics = db_job.metrics or {}
 670 |                 metrics["completed_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
 671 |                 db_job.metrics = metrics
 672 |                 db_job.status = "completed"
 673 |                 db.session.commit()
 674 |             
 675 |             # Publish completion
 676 |             publish_job_completed(
 677 |                 job_id,
 678 |                 view_url=f"/job/{job_id}/view",
 679 |                 result_files={
 680 |                     "tree_newick": f"/api/job/{job_id}/download/tree/newick",
 681 |                     "tree_nexus": f"/api/job/{job_id}/download/tree/nexus",
 682 |                     "fasta_original": f"/api/job/{job_id}/download/fasta/original",
 683 |                 }
 684 |             )
 685 |             publish_overview(job_id, "Pipeline complete! Redirecting to tree viewer...")
 686 | 
 687 |             return {
 688 |                 "job_id": job_id,
 689 |                 "status": "completed",
 690 |                 "job_dir": str(job_dir),
 691 |                 "message": "Job finished successfully",
 692 |                 "blast_result": blast_result,
 693 |                 "result_files": {
 694 |                     "alignment": str(alignment_trimmed_path),
 695 |                     "tree": str(tree_newick_path),
 696 |                     "tree_nexus": str(tree_nexus_path),
 697 |                     "metadata": str(tree_metadata_path),
 698 |                 },
 699 |             }
 700 | 
 701 |     except Exception as e:
 702 |         # =========================================================
 703 |         # CENTRALIZED ERROR HANDLING
 704 |         # =========================================================
 705 |         error_msg = str(e)
 706 |         tb = traceback.format_exc()
 707 |         
 708 |         logger.error(f"Job {job_id} failed at step '{current_step}': {error_msg}")
 709 |         logger.error(tb)
 710 |         
 711 |         # Get stderr tail from last command if available
 712 |         stderr_tail = last_stats.get("stderr_tail", [])
 713 |         exit_code = last_stats.get("exit_code")
 714 |         
 715 |         # Publish step failure
 716 |         if current_step:
 717 |             publish_step_failed(
 718 |                 job_id,
 719 |                 current_step,
 720 |                 error_msg,
 721 |                 tool=current_tool,
 722 |                 exit_code=exit_code,
 723 |                 stderr_tail=stderr_tail
 724 |             )
 725 |             update_step_meta(job, current_step, {"state": STATE_FAILED, "error": error_msg})
 726 |         
 727 |         # Publish job failure
 728 |         publish_job_failed(
 729 |             job_id,
 730 |             failed_step=current_step or "unknown",
 731 |             failed_step_label=current_step_label or "Unknown Step",
 732 |             error_summary=error_msg,
 733 |             tool=current_tool,
 734 |             exit_code=exit_code,
 735 |             stderr_tail=stderr_tail
 736 |         )
 737 |         
 738 |         # Update DB
 739 |         try:
 740 |             with _app.app_context():
 741 |                 db_job = Job.query.get(job_id)
 742 |                 if db_job:
 743 |                     metrics = db_job.metrics or {}
 744 |                     metrics["failed_at"] = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
 745 |                     metrics["error"] = error_msg
 746 |                     metrics["failed_step"] = current_step
 747 |                     db_job.metrics = metrics
 748 |                     db_job.status = "failed"
 749 |                     db.session.commit()
 750 |         except Exception as db_err:
 751 |             logger.error(f"Failed to update DB on error: {db_err}")
 752 |         
 753 |         # Re-raise so RQ marks job as failed
 754 |         raise
 755 | 
 756 |     finally:
 757 |         # Always remove the per-job handler so logs don't duplicate across jobs
 758 |         try:
 759 |             logger.removeHandler(file_handler)
 760 |             file_handler.close()
 761 |         except Exception:
 762 |             pass

FILE: app/workers/worker_monitor.py
========================================================================================

   1 | import os
   2 | import time
   3 | import socket
   4 | import logging
   5 | from pathlib import Path
   6 | from rq import Worker
   7 | 
   8 | logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   9 | 
  10 | class HeartbeatWorker(Worker):
  11 |     """
  12 |     A specific worker that writes a heartbeat file periodically.
  13 |     """
  14 |     def __init__(self, queues, name=None, default_result_ttl=None, connection=None,
  15 |                  exc_handler=None, default_worker_ttl=None, job_class=None,
  16 |                  queue_class=None, heartbeat_interval=10, worker_dir="/var/phylojobs/workers"):
  17 |         super().__init__(queues, name, default_result_ttl, connection,
  18 |                          exc_handler, default_worker_ttl, job_class, queue_class)
  19 |         self.heartbeat_interval = heartbeat_interval
  20 |         self.worker_dir = Path(worker_dir)
  21 |         self.last_heartbeat = 0
  22 |         
  23 |         # Ensure directory exists
  24 |         if not self.worker_dir.exists():
  25 |             try:
  26 |                 self.worker_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  27 |             except Exception as e:
  28 |                 logger.error(f"Could not create worker dir {self.worker_dir}: {e}")
  29 | 
  30 |     def work(self, burst=False, logging_level="INFO", date_format="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S", log_format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s: %(message)s", max_jobs=None, with_scheduler=False, **kwargs):
  31 |         """Override work to start heartbeat loop or hook into it."""
  32 |         # RQ's work loop is blocking. We can override `register_birth` or `monitor_work_horse`
  33 |         # But RQ doesn't make it super easy to run a side thread without custom loop.
  34 |         # simpler: override `heartbeat` method if available (used for redis), 
  35 |         # or just hook into the loop. 
  36 |         # Actually, RQ workers have a `work` loop that calls `play_work_horse`.
  37 |         # We can implement a method that updates the file.
  38 |         
  39 |         # We'll rely on the main loop calling `self.register_birth()` and `self.set_state()`.
  40 |         # But those update Redis. We want a file.
  41 |         # Let's wrap `register_birth` and `set_shutdown_requested_date` or similar?
  42 |         # A simple way is to extend `perform_job`? No, that's for jobs.
  43 |         
  44 |         # We can spawn a thread in __init__?
  45 |         import threading
  46 |         t = threading.Thread(target=self._file_heartbeat_loop, daemon=True)
  47 |         t.start()
  48 |         
  49 |         super().work(burst=burst, logging_level=logging_level, date_format=date_format, log_format=log_format, max_jobs=max_jobs, with_scheduler=with_scheduler, **kwargs)
  50 | 
  51 |     @staticmethod
  52 |     def _get_int_env(name: str, default: int) -> int:
  53 |         v = os.getenv(name, "")
  54 |         v = v.strip() if isinstance(v, str) else ""
  55 |         if not v:
  56 |             return default
  57 |         try:
  58 |             return int(v)
  59 |         except ValueError:
  60 |             return default
  61 | 
  62 | 
  63 |     def _file_heartbeat_loop(self):
  64 |         while True:
  65 |             try:
  66 |                 self.touch_heartbeat_file()
  67 |             except Exception as e:
  68 |                 logger.error(f"Heartbeat file error: {e}")
  69 |             time.sleep(self.heartbeat_interval)
  70 | 
  71 |     def touch_heartbeat_file(self):
  72 |         # file name: <hostname>.<pid>.<name>.heartbeat ?
  73 |         # Or just self.name (which is usually uuid)
  74 |         if not self.worker_dir.exists():
  75 |              return
  76 |              
  77 |         filepath = self.worker_dir / f"{self.name}.heartbeat"
  78 |         filepath.touch()
  79 |         
  80 |     def clean_up_heartbeat(self):
  81 |         filepath = self.worker_dir / f"{self.name}.heartbeat"
  82 |         if filepath.exists():
  83 |             filepath.unlink()
  84 | 
  85 | def run_worker_with_heartbeat(app):
  86 |     """
  87 |     Entry point to run this worker.
  88 |     """
  89 |     from app.workers.queue import get_queue
  90 |     from app.workers.queue import get_redis_connection
  91 |     
  92 |     with app.app_context():
  93 |         conn = get_redis_connection()
  94 |         # Default queue
  95 |         queue = get_queue() # returns Queue object
  96 |         maintenance_interval = HeartbeatWorker._get_int_env("RQ_MAINTENANCE_INTERVAL", 600)
  97 |         result_ttl = HeartbeatWorker._get_int_env("RQ_RESULT_TTL", 86400)  # 1 day default
  98 |         # We need to pass queues list
  99 |         worker = HeartbeatWorker([queue], connection=conn, 
 100 |                                  worker_dir=app.config.get("WORKER_DIR", "var/workers"), default_result_ttl=result_ttl)
 101 | 
 102 |         worker.maintenance_interval = maintenance_interval
 103 |         
 104 |         # Cleanup on exit
 105 |         try:
 106 |             worker.work()
 107 |         finally:
 108 |             worker.clean_up_heartbeat()

FILE: ARCHITECTURE.md
========================================================================================

   1 | # Application Architecture
   2 | 
   3 | ## High-Level Overview
   4 | This is a Flask-based bioinformatics application designed to handle sequence analysis jobs. It uses an asynchronous architecture to process long-running tasks like sequence alignment and phylogenetic tree building.
   5 | 
   6 | ## Technology Stack
   7 | 
   8 | ### Core Frameworks
   9 | - **Web Framework**: Flask
  10 | - **WSGI Server**: Gunicorn
  11 | - **Language**: Python 3
  12 | 
  13 | ### Data & State
  14 | - **Database**: PostgreSQL (via `psycopg2-binary`)
  15 | - **ORM**: Flask-SQLAlchemy
  16 | - **Migrations**: Flask-Migrate (Alembic)
  17 | - **Queue/Cache**: Redis
  18 | 
  19 | ### Asynchronous Processing
  20 | - **Queue Manager**: RQ (Redis Queue)
  21 | - **Monitoring**: `psutil` (system resource monitoring)
  22 | 
  23 | ### Bioinformatics
  24 | - **Library**: Biopython
  25 | - **Job Types**:
  26 |     - Sequence Orientation (auto-fix reversed ITS sequences)
  27 |     - Sequence Alignment (supported methods: MAFFT, MUSCLE, ClustalO, etc.)
  28 |     - Tree Building (supported methods: NJ, RAxML, IQ-TREE, MrBayes)
  29 |     - Trimming (supported methods: trimAl, BMGE)
  30 | 
  31 | ### Authentication
  32 | - **Library**: Flask-Login
  33 | - **Security**: Werkzeug password hashing
  34 | 
  35 | ## Database Schema
  36 | The application uses a relational database model defined in `app/models.py`.
  37 | 
  38 | ### Models
  39 | - **User**
  40 |     - `id`: Primary Key
  41 |     - `email`: Unique identifier
  42 |     - `password_hash`: Hashed password
  43 |     - `created_at`: Timestamp
  44 |     - *Relationships*: One-to-Many with `Job`
  45 | 
  46 | - **Job**
  47 |     - `id`: Primary Key (String, likely UUID)
  48 |     - `user_id`: Foreign Key to `User`
  49 |     - `status`: Job state (e.g., "queued", "running", "completed", "failed")
  50 |     - `input_type`: Type of input data
  51 |     - `job_dir`: File system path to job results
  52 |     - `metrics`: JSON field for storing job-specific metrics
  53 |     - `created_at` / `updated_at`: Timestamps
  54 | 
  55 | ### Data Classes
  56 | The application also defines several `dataclass` structures for handling job parameters (not stored directly as separate tables, likely serialized or used for validation):
  57 | - `AlignmentParams`
  58 | - `TrimmingParams`
  59 | - `TreeBuilderParams`
  60 | - `JobParams`
  61 | 
  62 | ## Application Structure (`app/`)
  63 | The application follows a modular factory pattern. Below is a detailed breakdown of the file structure:
  64 | 
  65 | ### 1. Configuration & Core
  66 | - `app/__init__.py`: **Application Factory**. Initializes the Flask app, registers extensions and blueprints.
  67 | - `app/config.py`: Configuration classes (Development, Production) loading settings from environment variables.
  68 | - `app/extensions.py`: Initialization of Flask extensions (`db`, `login_manager`, `migrate`, `redis_client`).
  69 | - `app/cli.py`: Custom Flask CLI commands (e.g., `flask run-worker`, `flask run-metrics`).
  70 | - `app/models.py`: Database models (`User`, `Job`) and data classes (`AlignmentParams`, etc.).
  71 | 
  72 | ### 2. Blueprints (Route Logic)
  73 | Each blueprint encapsulates a domain of the application:
  74 | - **`app/main/`**: Core application logic.
  75 |     - `routes.py`: General routes (landing page, static pages).
  76 | - **`app/auth/`**: Authentication.
  77 |     - `routes.py`: Login, logout, registration flows.
  78 | - **`app/user/`**: User-centric views.
  79 |     - `routes.py`: User dashboard, job list (`/user/jobs`).
  80 | - **`app/api/`**: REST API for frontend interactions.
  81 |     - `routes.py`: Endpoints for job status, data download, and tree operations.
  82 | - **`app/monitoring/`**: System health.
  83 |     - `services.py`: Logic for collecting system metrics (`psutil`).
  84 |     - `routes.py`: Dashboard for viewing system load.
  85 | 
  86 | ### 3. Services (Business Logic)
  87 | Encapsulates complex logic, separated from route handlers:
  88 | - `app/services/alignment_service.py`: Handles sequence alignment (MAFFT, MUSCLE, etc.).
  89 | - `app/services/tree_builder_service.py`: Handles phylogenetic tree inference (RAxML, IQ-TREE).
  90 | - `app/services/trimming_service.py`: Sequence trimming (trimAl).
  91 | - `app/services/tree_edit_service.py`: Tree manipulation logic (rerooting, pruning, node renaming).
  92 | - `app/services/blast_service.py`: Integration with BLAST tools.
  93 | - `app/services/subprocess_utils.py`: Utilities for safely execution shell commands.
  94 | 
  95 | ### 4. Background Workers (`app/workers/`)
  96 | Handles asynchronous task processing using Redis Queue (RQ):
  97 | - `tasks.py`: Entry points for background jobs (e.g., `run_alignment`, `run_tree_building`).
  98 | - `queue.py`: Helper functions to enqueue jobs.
  99 | - `worker_monitor.py`: Logic to monitor worker health and heartbeat.
 100 | 
 101 | ### 5. Frontend (`app/templates/` & `app/static/`)
 102 | #### Templates (Jinja2)
 103 | - **Base Layouts**:
 104 |     - `base.html`: Main layout wrapper.
 105 |     - `index.html`: Home page.
 106 | - **Job Views**:
 107 |     - `user_jobs.html`: List of user's submitted jobs.
 108 |     - `job_viewer.html`: Detailed view of a specific job (results).
 109 |     - `job_status.html`: Current status of a running job.
 110 | - **Partials**:
 111 |     - `partials/viewer_controls.html`: Control panel for tree viewers.
 112 |     - `partials/phylotree_viewer.html`: Container for Phylotree.js.
 113 | - **Admin**:
 114 |     - `admin/monitoring.html`: System metrics dashboard.
 115 | 
 116 | #### Static Assets
 117 | - **CSS** (`app/static/css/`):
 118 |     - `style.css`: Global styles.
 119 |     - `tree_viewer.css`: Specific styles for the tree visualization.
 120 | - **JavaScript** (`app/static/js/`):
 121 |     - `tree_viewer_controller.js`: **Main Controller**. Orchestrates the tree viewer UI.
 122 |     - `tree_viewer_api.js`: Handles AJAX requests to module API.
 123 |     - `tree_edit_actions.js`: Bridges UI actions to API calls (prune, reroot).
 124 |     - `phylotree.js` / `tree_viewer_phylotree_v2.js`: D3-based tree rendering logic.
 125 | 
 126 | ## Runtime & Deployment
 127 | - **Environment**: Remote Linux Server
 128 | - **Virtual Environment**: `/var/www/dikarya/.venv`
 129 | - **Entry Point**: `wsgi.py`
 130 | - **System Services (Systemd)**:
 131 |     - `dikarya-web.service`: The web application server
 132 |     - `dikarya-worker.service`: Asynchronous task worker
 133 |     - **Note**: Service runs on `https://127.0.0.1:8000`
 134 | 
 135 | ## Development Workflow
 136 | 
 137 | ### Web Application
 138 | To run the app in development mode with hot-reloading:
 139 | 
 140 | ```bash
 141 | export FLASK_APP=wsgi.py
 142 | export FLASK_ENV=development
 143 | flask run --port=5000
 144 | ```
 145 | 
 146 | ### Worker
 147 | To run the worker in development mode:
 148 | 
 149 | ```bash
 150 | rq worker -u redis://localhost:6379 dikarya-tasks
 151 | ```
 152 | 
 153 | ## Debugging & Logging
 154 | 
 155 | ### Logs
 156 | - **Error Log**: `/var/www/dikarya/var/logs/error.log`
 157 | - **Access Log**: `/var/www/dikarya/var/logs/access.log`
 158 | 
 159 | ### Troubleshooting
 160 | - **500 Server Errors**: Check the error log for stack traces.
 161 | - **Route Changes**: If you add or modify routes (e.g., adding `user.clear_jobs`), you **MUST** reload the Gunicorn server for changes to take effect.
 162 |     - Find the Master PID: `ps aux | grep gunicorn`
 163 |     - Send HUP signal: `kill -HUP <MASTER_PID>`
